We also measured the correlation between the synthetic data quality metrics with downstream nuclei segmentation performance using common metrics including the aggregated Jaccard index (AJI+) and the Dice coefficient. The study results suggest that pathology-specific metrics may provide improved discriminative power. Specifically, the modified Inception Score indicates higher correlation with downstream task performance (r=0.6096 with AJI+, p=0.0122), compared to the original IS (r=0.0708, p=0.7944). Our observations indicate that increasing the variety of generated training data has a higher positive correlation with segmentation model performance than improving the visual fidelity of individual generated images.

</details>


### [104] [Test-Time Scaling in Reasoning LLMs: Inference Regimes, Evaluation, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04001)
*Mohsen Hariri,Weicong Chen,Nahal Shahini,Vikash Singh,Kai Ye,Amirhossein Samandar,Debargha Ganguly,Sreehari Sankar,Yanyan Zhang,Shouren Wang,Jerry Peng,Biyao Zhang,Michael Hinczewski,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统地研究了测试时扩展（test-time scaling）问题，提出了沿三个轴线的形式化框架：推断的统计结构（单轨迹、叶级、前缀级）、评估原则（端到端系统性能与候选库诊断分离）、以及可复现性要求（精确重放与分布可复现）。作者还发布了超过20亿条完整推理轨迹数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前"测试时扩展"一词涵盖多种不同的推断算法（单轨迹延展、采样聚合、搜索未完成状态），这些算法在统计结构、计算核算和失败模式上存在本质差异，但被统一当作可互换的标量"预算"处理，导致不同研究结果难以比较。

Method: 作者开发了测试时扩展的系统性阐述框架：1）将测试时扩展形式化为自回归模型隐式前缀树上的预算推断，区分三种结构模式（单轨迹顺序扩展、叶级扩展与终端归约、前缀级扩展）；2）将评估对象视为整个推断系统，提出分离端到端系统性能与候选库诊断的评估原则，引入评估档案（evaluation profile）；3）制定推断协议的可复现性要求，区分精确重放与分布可复现性。同时组织开源权重推理生态系统、在多个基准上应用这些原则，并发布大规模推理轨迹数据集。

Result: 作者提出了一个多维度的测试时扩展分析框架，其评估档案的坐标和简单泛函能够恢复或界定常见的重复采样指标，规定了协议匹配的计算和不确定性报告方式，并在广泛知识、符号推理和竞赛数学基准上验证了这些原则，最终组装并发布了包含超过20亿条完整推理轨迹的数据集（附带逐步丰富的验证器和token级别信号）。

Conclusion: 测试时扩展的研究需要超越单一标量"预算"的简化视角，而应采用结构感知的形式化框架来比较不同推断算法；评估应面向整个推断系统而非仅报告准确性；可复现性要求应明确区分精确重放与分布可复现两种层次，并为开源推理生态提供标准化的报告和评估实践。

Abstract: Large language models can solve substantially harder reasoning problems with more inference-time compute. The term "test-time scaling," however, now covers diverse inference algorithms that extend deliberation along a single trajectory, sample completed candidates and aggregate them through voting or verification, or search over unfinished partial states. These algorithms differ in their statistical structure, compute accounting, and failure modes. Treating these procedures as interchangeable under a single scalar "budget," or reporting accuracy without the inference protocol that produced it, makes results difficult to compare across studies. We develop a systematic account of test-time scaling along three axes. First, we formalize test-time scaling as budgeted inference over the implicit prefix tree of an autoregressive model and distinguish three structural regimes: single-trajectory sequential scaling, leaf-level scaling with terminal reduction, and prefix-level scaling. Second, we treat the evaluated object as the entire inference system and develop evaluation principles that separate end-to-end system performance from candidate-bank diagnostics. We introduce an evaluation profile whose coordinates and simple functionals recover or bound common repeated-sampling metrics, and prescribe protocol-matched reporting of compute and uncertainty. Third, we specify reproducibility requirements for inference protocols, distinguishing exact replay from distributional reproducibility and identifying the artifacts needed to support each. We also organize the open-weight reasoning ecosystem by model-side and interface mechanisms, apply these principles to broad-knowledge, symbolic-reasoning, and competition-mathematics benchmarks, and assemble over 2 billion full reasoning traces for release with progressively richer verifier and token-level signals.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Knowledge-Geometry Decoupling: Refreshable Pretrained Transfer for Streaming Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02738)
*Zixuan Wang,Yuhong Chen,Yuxuan Zhu,Guidong Lei,Zhiluohan Guo,Yu Zhao,Kun Wang,Bangyang Hong,Kangle Wu,Yabo Ni,Anxiang Zeng,Cong Fu,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出知识几何解耦（KGD）框架，解决工业推荐系统中预训练-迁移范式下行为分布漂移带来的两个问题：行为序列学什么以及知识如何持续迁移。KGD通过行为多Token预测（BMTP）保留更干净的可迁移行为知识，并通过参数解耦实现持续知识刷新而不干扰下游任务。在8个公共基准上提升4-12%，在Shopee首页搜索的在线A/B测试中GMV提升1.75%、广告收入提升1.53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统普遍采用预训练-再迁移范式，但行为分布漂移带来两个核心挑战：一是传统的Next-Token预测将相邻关系视为依赖关系，可能编码跨无关会话的虚假转换，导致行为知识不干净；二是预训练知识与任务特定几何对共享参数施加冲突的优化需求，使得模型持续刷新时难以兼顾知识更新与下游适应。

Method: 提出KGD（知识几何解耦）框架，包含两大部分：(1) 行为多Token预测（BMTP）：不再对所有后续token进行预测，而是仅保留协同过滤或语义上相关的未来项目作为监督信号，从而获得更干净且更具可迁移性的行为知识；(2) 参数解耦架构：将预训练知识与任务特定几何分配给不同参数集——可刷新的编码器拥有行为知识，任务学习器通过只读交叉注意力读取上下文化的编码器状态，并通过与预训练嵌入正交的锚定校准残差（ACR）写入任务特定几何。这种解耦所有权使得知识持续刷新时不会受任务梯度干扰，也不会使下游适配失效。

Result: KGD在8个公共基准上相比强预训练-迁移基线提升4-12%；在90天的生产数据流上相比基线保持持续优势（基线无增益）；已在Shopee全面部署，首页搜索的在线A/B测试显示每位用户GMV提升1.75%，广告收入提升1.53%。

Conclusion: KGD通过回答"行为序列学什么"（只学相关监督而非所有相邻标记）和"如何持续迁移"（预训练知识与任务几何参数解耦）两个问题，有效提升了预训练-迁移范式在工业推荐系统的鲁棒性与持续学习能力，并已在生产环境中验证了显著的商业价值。

Abstract: Industrial recommenders increasingly adopt the pretrain-then-transfer paradigm, yet behavioral distribution drift raises two questions: what to learn from behavior sequences, and how to transfer the learned knowledge while the pretrained model is continually refreshed. To resolve them, we propose Knowledge-Geometry Decoupling (KGD). For what to learn, conventional next-token prediction treats adjacency as dependency and may encode spurious transitions across unrelated sessions. We introduce Behavioral Multi-Token Prediction (BMTP) to retain only collaboratively or semantically related future items as supervision, yielding cleaner and more transferable behavioral knowledge. For how to transfer, pretrained knowledge and task-specific geometry impose conflicting optimization demands on shared parameters. To handle it, KGD assigns them to separate parameter sets: a refreshable encoder owns behavioral knowledge, while a task learner reads contextualized encoder states through read-only cross-attention and writes task-specific geometry through Anchored Calibration Residual (ACR) orthogonal to the pretrained embedding. The decoupled ownership enables continual knowledge refresh without task-gradient interference or invalidating downstream adaptation. KGD improves over strong pretrain-transfer baselines by 4-12% on eight public benchmarks and sustains its advantage over a 90-day production stream where baselines show no gains. KGD has been fully deployed in Shopee. In a live A/B test on Shopee Homepage Search, it increases GMV per user by 1.75% and advertising revenue by 1.53%, demonstrating its high practical value. We provide the core implementation of KGD at https://github.com/FuCongResearchSquad/KGD4REC.

</details>


### [106] [Search, Inspect, Fetch: Exploiting Boolean Retrieval for Deep-Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02751)
*Shuai Wang,Haodong Chen,Yu Yin,Shengyao Zhuang,Bevan Koopman,Guido Zuccon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SIEVE，一种基于字段化布尔检索（BQL）的搜索-检查-获取界面，允许智能体直接按文档字段（标题、标题、章节、元数据）筛选和获取内容，在三个QA数据集上相比传统搜索-访问方法在更高准确率的同时减少了20.7-50.6%的token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究智能体采用搜索-访问工作流，只检索和阅读整个页面，未利用网页的字段化结构（标题、章节、元数据等），导致无法将检索约束到特定文档字段，并经常将无关页面内容带入上下文。

Method: SIEVE采用搜索-检查-获取（search-inspect-fetch）接口，基于字段化布尔检索（BQL）：先在文档字段上过滤候选文档，再对筛选后的集合排序，呈现结构丰富的结果卡片供检查，最后仅获取选中的章节内容。

Result: 在三个QA数据集上，SIEVE在准确率上均超过最准确的常规搜索-访问配置，同时使用更少的token（减少20.7-50.6%）。BQL过滤能提升所有测试的排序器性能，且这种准确率-上下文优势在多种检索器和智能体主干上保持一致。

Conclusion: 利用文档字段化结构进行字段级过滤和分节获取，能够显著提升深度研究智能体的准确率与推理效率，且该优势具有较好的泛化性。代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Existing deep-research agents use a search-visit workflow that retrieves and reads whole pages, without considering the addressable structure that web sources expose through titles, headings, sections, and metadata. This prevents agents from directly constraining retrieval to document fields and often carries irrelevant page content into their context. We introduce SIEVE, a search-inspect-fetch interface driven by fielded Boolean retrieval (BQL). SIEVE filters candidates over document fields, ranks the admitted set, presents structure-rich result cards for inspection, and fetches only selected sections. Across three QA collections, SIEVE achieves higher accuracy than the most accurate conventional Search-Visit configuration on each collection while using 20.7-50.6% fewer tokens. Further analyses show that BQL filtering improves all tested rankers and that the accuracy-context advantage persists across retriever choices and agent backbones. Code and data are available at https://github.com/ielab/skim-search-agent.

</details>


### [107] [Field Aware Agent Skill Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02880)
*Paimon Goulart,Liang Wu,Kelly Wan,Evangelos E. Papalexakis,Liangjie Hong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究终身学习智能体在技能库不断增长时，如何通过保留技能的多字段结构（而非扁平化拼接）来提升技能检索效果。将每个技能拆分为独立组件，分别计算各字段的稀疏和稠密相似度，再通过均匀权重或小型MLP结合字段级分数，在SkillRet和SRA-Bench两个基准上验证了字段感知检索的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着终身学习智能体的技能库持续增长，准确检索正确技能成为关键瓶颈。现有方法将每个技能视作一个扁平文档（拼接名称、描述、正文等字段），忽略了技能天然是多字段、结构化的对象，每个字段提供不同维度的使用时机和方式信息。

Method: 将每个技能表示为其独立的各个组件（字段），对每个字段独立计算稀疏和稠密相似度，形成张量化的字段感知技能库表示。然后通过两种方式组合字段级分数：均匀权重或小型学习的MLP。

Result: 字段感知MLP在SkillRet上达到77.95的Recall@10，在SRA-Bench上达到83.78的Recall@10，优于对应的拼接学习的基线方法。且随着技能库规模增大，字段感知检索的优势更加明显。

Conclusion: 技能表示本身对检索效果至关重要，简单地保留技能文件中已有的结构就能显著提升检索性能，尤其在检索最困难的超大技能库场景下优势更为突出。

Abstract: As lifelong learning agents accumulate lifelong growing skill banks, retrieving the correct skill becomes an increasingly important bottleneck. Most current skill retrieval methods treat each skill as one flat document by concatenating fields such as the name, description, and body. However, skills are naturally structured, multi-field objects, where each field provides different information about when and how the skill should be used. In this work, we study whether preserving this structure improves skill retrieval. We represent each skill as its separate components, and compute sparse and dense similarities for each field independently, exposing a naturally tensorized, field-aware representation of the skill bank. We then combine these field-level scores either with uniform weights or with a small learned MLP. Across two different skill retrieval benchmarks, SkillRet and SRA-Bench, we find that keeping fields separate improves hybrid retrieval, and learning over the field-level scores gives the strongest and most consistent results. Our field-aware MLP reaches $77.95$ Recall@10 on SkillRet and $83.78$ Recall@10 on SRA-Bench, outperforming the corresponding concatenated learned baselines. We also find that the advantage grows as the skill bank becomes larger, suggesting that field-aware skill retrieval becomes especially useful in the setting where retrieval is most difficult. Our results show that skill representation itself matters, and that simply preserving the structure already present in skill files can substantially improve retrieval.

</details>


### [108] [Coverage Matters: MarginMerge for Compressing Multi-Vector Visual Document Retrievers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02969)
*Ailar Mahdizadeh,Aria Salari,Sohail Rajabi,Shahriar Mirabbasi,Panos Nasiopoulos,Alireza Morsali*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MarginMerge，一种针对冻结多向量视觉文档检索器的压缩方法，通过选择覆盖感知锚点、聚类文档补丁并用轻量共享网络合成每类代表向量，在5%-10%向量保留率下保持97%-99%的nDCG@5，同时降低90%-95%的存储向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多向量视觉文档检索器（如ColPali、ColQwen）虽检索性能强，但存储大量细粒度补丁嵌入导致索引庞大、后期交互评分代价高。现有压缩方法常独立按显著性选择补丁，忽略了查询相关覆盖度，未能保留跨查询可能成为最强MaxSim匹配的多样化文档区域。

Method: 提出MarginMerge方法：1）选择覆盖感知的锚点（coverage-aware anchors）；2）对文档补丁进行聚类；3）使用轻量共享网络合成每个聚类的代表性向量。压缩在索引阶段一次性完成，检索阶段保持标准MaxSim接口不变。

Result: 在六个数据集上（ColQwen2.5和ColPali两个骨干模型），MarginMerge在5%和10%向量保留率下达到最高的查询无关平均匹配度。相比未压缩索引，保持97%-99%的平均nDCG@5，同时减少90%-95%的存储文档向量。在5%保留率下，相比几何融合压缩方法，在全部六个ColQwen2.5数据集上平均减少约41%的排序翻转。模型无需重训练即可迁移到未见数据集和保留率。

Conclusion: 有效的向量压缩应保留查询相关的覆盖度（即跨查询可能成为最强MaxSim匹配的多样化文档区域），而非独立按显著性选择补丁。这一视角同时解释了为何密集渲染页面比自然图像更易压缩。MarginMerge在极大压缩下仍保持高检索性能，且具有良好的泛化性和迁移性。

Abstract: Multi-vector visual document retrievers such as ColPali and ColQwen achieve strong retrieval by storing fine-grained patch embeddings, but this produces large indexes and costly late-interaction scoring. We argue that effective compression should preserve query-relevant coverage, meaning the diverse document regions that may become the strongest MaxSim match across queries, rather than selecting patches independently by salience. This view also explains why dense rendered pages are easier to compress than natural images. We introduce MarginMerge, a compression method for frozen multi-vector retrievers. It selects coverage-aware anchors, clusters document patches, and uses a lightweight shared network to synthesize one representative per cluster. Compression is performed once during indexing, while retrieval keeps the standard MaxSim interface. Across six datasets on both ColQwen2.5 and ColPali, MarginMerge achieves the highest matched query-agnostic average at 5% and 10% vector retention. Compared with the uncompressed index using the same backbone, it preserves between 97% and 99% of average nDCG@5 while reducing stored document vectors by between 90% and 95%. At 5% retention, it also reduces ranking flips relative to geometric merging on all six ColQwen2.5 datasets by approximately 41% on average. The same model transfers to unseen datasets and retention ratios without retraining.

</details>


### [109] [Position Bias Undermines Preference Consistency in Listwise LLM-Based Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03091)
*Ethan Bito,Yongli Ren,Estrid He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型（LLM）作为推荐系统列表重排序器时，对等效候选排列的位置偏差问题。作者提出一个评估框架，从成对偏好不稳定性、全局偏好不一致性和列表输出一致性三个层面刻画候选顺序敏感性，实验表明这些度量虽相互关联，但与推荐效果和边际位置暴露偏差可能脱节，仅减少暴露偏差不足以确保重排序函数的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM重排序器在处理无序候选集合时，输出结果可能依赖于候选的序列化顺序，但现有研究主要关注最终排序列表的变化，缺乏对排序过程内部偏好结构的系统性评估。作者希望通过更细粒度的度量来理解位置偏差如何影响LLM重排序的可靠性。

Method: 提出一个三层次评估框架：(1) 成对偏好不稳定性——衡量等效排列下候选对相对偏好是否翻转；(2) 全局偏好不一致性——考察整体偏好结构是否自洽；(3) 列表输出一致性——评估最终排序列表的稳定程度。将等效排列下产生的排序视为一个诱导偏好系统的观测，跨多个LLM、数据集和列表长度进行实验。

Result: 实验发现：三种一致性度量之间高度吻合，但可能与推荐有效性和边际位置暴露偏差出现分歧。即使提升相关性或使各位置的暴露分布更平坦，也不一定能恢复稳定的成对偏好、全局一致的偏好结构或一致的排序输出。

Conclusion: 仅关注并减少边际暴露偏差不足以确立LLM重排序的排名函数有效性。需要在更深的偏好层次上审视和修正候选顺序敏感性，以确保重排序结果对无序候选集真正可靠。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as promising listwise rerankers for recommender systems, but their reliability under equivalent candidate permutations remains unclear. Since recommendation candidates form an unordered set, a reranker should not depend on the arbitrary order used to serialize them. However, decoder-only LLM rerankers can allow input order to affect model scores, pairwise preferences, and rankings. We study how position bias affects the ranking process induced by LLM-based rerankers. Instead of measuring only changes in final ranked lists, we treat rankings produced under equivalent candidate permutations as observations of an induced preference system. We introduce an evaluation framework measuring pairwise preference instability, global preference inconsistency, and listwise output consistency. This framework characterizes candidate-order sensitivity at the pairwise, global, and output levels. Experiments across multiple LLMs, datasets, and list lengths show that these consistency measures are closely aligned, but can diverge from recommendation effectiveness and marginal position-exposure bias. Improving relevance or flattening exposure across positions does not necessarily restore stable pairwise preferences, globally coherent preference structures, or consistent ranked outputs. These results show that reducing marginal exposure skew is insufficient to establish ranking-function validity in LLM-based reranking. Code is available at https://github.com/ejbito/InvariRank .

</details>


### [110] [Attacking and Defending Multi-Agent Collaborative Filtering Systems Through Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03272)
*Anjun Hu,Hanting Xie,Saranya Govindan,Jas Kandola,Kurt Cutajar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体协同过滤（CF）系统中，由LLM驱动的用户和项目智能体通过自然语言交互进行偏好协商时的安全漏洞问题。研究在AgentCF框架下系统性地改变连接性（包括候选数量和目录集中度两个维度），评估了从多智能体系统文献中改编的攻击与防御方法，揭示了用户/项目智能体的角色不对称性、攻击效能的非单调时间动态以及不同攻击目标（传播vs提取）的差异模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体CF系统同时继承了数据驱动和多智能体交互的漏洞，但连接性如何调节这些系统的脆弱性尚未被充分理解。理解这种关系有助于开发更稳健的推荐流水线。

Method: 在AgentCF框架下，沿两个连接性维度（候选数量和目录集中度）系统性地变化设置，改编并复现MAS文献中的攻击与防御方法，评估其效果；同时探索使用流行病学启发的静态指标来排序CF配置的预期攻击结果。

Result: 确认了MAS攻击和防御在智能体CF领域的部分可迁移性；揭示了用户与项目智能体间的角色不对称性、攻击效能的非单调时间动态，以及传播目标和提取目标之间的不同模式。流行病学静态指标在排序CF配置风险方面具有潜在应用价值。

Conclusion: 连接性的两个维度（候选数量和目录集中度）对攻击与防御结果有显著影响，且影响模式复杂（非单调、不对称）。流行病学启发的静态指标可作为成本高效的鲁棒性评估手段，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-agent collaborative filtering (CF) systems coordinate autonomous LLM-powered user and item agents through natural-language interaction to refine preferences and generate recommendations. These systems inherit vulnerabilities from both their data-driven nature and their multi-agent interactions, which manifest in distinct ways. Understanding how connectivity modulates vulnerability in these systems could facilitate the development of more robust recommendation pipelines.
  In this work, we adapt attacks and defenses from the general multi-agent systems (MAS) literature to the agent-based CF setting, evaluating them under systematically varied connectivity in the AgentCF framework, where CF connectivity is characterized along two axes: (i) candidate count (the number of item candidates per turn per user, measuring user-side interaction density) and (ii) catalog concentration (the degree of item catalog overlap across users).
  Our contributions include: (1) Adaptation: we reproduce MAS-inspired attacks and defenses in the agentic CF domain, confirming partial transferability of original observations. (2) Characterization: we characterize how the two aspects of connectivity shape attack and defense outcomes, revealing role asymmetries between user and item agents, non-monotonic temporal dynamics in attack efficacy, and divergent patterns across dissemination and extraction attack goals. Additionally, as an exploratory extension, we assess the applicability of epidemic-inspired static metrics in ranking CF configurations by expected attack outcome, potentially enabling cost-efficient robustness assessment. Implementation is available at https://github.com/anjunhu/ConnACF

</details>


### [111] [LLM-Derived Priors for Thompson Sampling in Cold-Start Comment Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03382)
*Eugene Lee,Oseong Choi,Byungsoo Kang,Taeyeong Jang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文利用大语言模型（LLM）从用户生成的评论文本中提取语义信号，转化为贝叶斯先验知识，以热启动Thompson采样算法，解决在线推荐系统中新物品缺乏交互历史导致的冷启动问题，并在真实线上A/B/C实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多臂老虎机算法（尤其是Thompson采样）广泛用于在线推荐，但新加入的臂（本文中指用户生成的文本评论）因缺乏交互历史而面临冷启动问题，需要利用评论文本的语义内容来弥补早期反馈不足的缺陷。

Method: 使用LLM从评论文本中提取语义信号，将其转化为信息丰富的贝叶斯先验来热启动Thompson采样；同时针对每个性别-年龄细分人群分别维护和更新后验分布，以处理不同细分人群的响应模式差异。在真实线上A/B/C测试中，对比了均匀先验与两种基于LLM的先验设计（Gender Prior和Content Prior）。

Result: LLM先验在稀疏反馈场景下收益最大，边际收益在积累了少量交互证据后最为显著；不同的先验设计产生不同的漏斗效应。点击导向的对齐性在Gender Prior下最强，且处理效应在不同人口统计细分人群中差异显著。

Conclusion: LLM派生的先验可作为文本丰富场景下老虎机推荐系统的实用热启动机制，但部署时需要权衡先验设计的选择及其对不同人群的影响。

Abstract: Multi-armed bandit algorithms, especially Thompson sampling, are widely used in online recommendation. Despite their ability to adapt from online feedback, these methods often suffer from cold-start limitations when newly introduced arms have little or no interaction history. In our setting, the candidate arms are user-generated textual comments, whose semantic content can reveal a title's appeal before sufficient interaction feedback is available. We therefore use large language models (LLMs) to extract semantic signals from comment text and convert them into informative Bayesian priors that warm-start Thompson sampling under sparse early-stage feedback. To account for aggregate segment-level differences in response patterns, we maintain and update posteriors separately for each gender-age segment. In a real-world online A/B/C test, we compare a uniform prior with two LLM-based designs: a Gender Prior for demographic-affinity cues and a Content Prior for title-specific identity cues. The results show that LLM-based priors are most beneficial in sparse-feedback regimes -- with the largest gains emerging once a small amount of interaction evidence has accumulated -- and that prior design leads to distinct funnel-level effects. We further analyze prior-reward alignment and demographic heterogeneity, finding that click-oriented alignment is strongest for the Gender Prior and that treatment effects vary substantially across demographic segments. These findings suggest that LLM-derived priors can serve as a practical warm-start mechanism for text-rich bandit recommendation, while also revealing deployment trade-offs.

</details>


### [112] [Training Documents Reranker with Search Rubrics for Deep Research Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03527)
*Wenhan Liu,Yu Lu,Qiaolin Xia,Hui Xu,Tong Zhao,Jian Xi,Yutao Zhu,Haijin Liang,Haibo Shi,Hao Wang,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RubricRanker，一种基于搜索导向评分标准的文档重排序器，用于为深度研究代理选择高质量文档集合，在四个深度研究基准上超过最强基线2.6个点，并在五个RAG基准上表现出良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索器通常通过相关性匹配选择文档，但个别匹配良好的top-k文档组合在一起可能无法满足代理查询的复杂信息需求（如多样性、简洁性、权威性），需要显式定义高质量文档集合的标准。

Method: 提出搜索导向评分标准（search-oriented rubrics），以层级结构组织并用强大LLM合成，显式定义每个代理查询下高质量文档集合应满足的要求。基于这些评分标准训练文档重排序器RubricRanker，采用两阶段训练框架：评分标准引导的监督微调与基于评分标准的强化学习。

Result: RubricRanker在四个深度研究基准上超过最强基线2.6个点，并在五个RAG基准上表现良好，展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 显式定义搜索评分标准并据此训练重排序器，可有效改善深度研究代理的文档集合选取质量，超越传统相关性匹配方法。

Abstract: Retrieval systems help deep research agents generate high-quality answers by providing relevant documents. However, existing retrievers typically select documents through relevance matching, while individually well-matched top-$k$ documents may not form a \textit{set} that satisfies the complex information needs of an agent query (\eg, diverse, concise and authoritative documents). In this paper, we propose search-oriented rubrics that \textit{explicitly} define the requirements that high-quality document sets should satisfy for each agent query. Our search rubrics are organized into a hierarchical structure and synthesized using a powerful LLM. Based on these search rubrics, we further train a document reranker \textbf{RubricRanker} to select a high-quality subset from retrieved documents. We design a two-stage training framework that consists of rubrics-guided supervised fine-tuning and rubric-based reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RubricRanker outperforms the strongest baseline by 2.6 points on four deep research benchmarks and generalizes well to five RAG benchmarks.

</details>


### [113] [Conditionally Identifiable Latent-Environment Modeling for Out-of-Distribution Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03647)
*Qianqian Wang,Wenwu Gong,Yunshan Li,Zhenqing Wu,Ruili Wang,Lili Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CILER框架，用于条件可识别的风险感知推荐系统，通过用户条件指数族建模潜在环境，实现OOD推荐中的偏好偏移识别与预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD推荐方法虽然能从历史交互中推断潜在环境状态，但潜在环境的统计含义及其对偏好的影响机制仍然不明确，导致模型的可解释性和可靠性不足。

Method: CILER采用用户条件指数族对潜在环境建模，使用特征索引多项式刻画环境对偏好的影响方式，并通过在推断出的环境分布上对项目概率进行边际化来做出预测。

Result: 在三个数据集上，CILER在特征偏移、时间偏移和地理偏移三类共享支撑下的OOD场景中，全部十二项排序评估指标均获得提升。同时提供了充分变化条件和模型设定正确性下的可识别性保证，以及部署风险的误差上界。

Conclusion: CILER在理论上确保了环境敏感表示的可识别性（直至等价类），在实践中显著提升了OOD推荐性能，为风险感知推荐提供了理论与实证兼备的解决方案。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) recommendation is vulnerable to preference shifts induced by a latent environment. Existing methods can infer latent states from logged interactions, yet the statistical meaning of the latent environment and its effect on preference remain underdetermined. We formulate this task as conditionally identifiable risk-aware recommendation (CI-RR) and propose Conditionally Identifiable Latent-Environment Recommendation (CILER). CILER uses a user-conditioned exponential family to model the latent environment and a feature-indexed polynomial to specify how it changes preference. It predicts by marginalizing item probabilities over the inferred environment distribution. Under sufficient variation, correct specification, and decoder regularity, CILER identifies the environment-sensitive representation up to the stated equivalence class. We further bound excess deployment log-risk by environment-inference error. Controlled studies test the observable consequences of sufficient variation and model specification. Experiments on three datasets show that CILER improves all twelve OOD ranking metrics under feature, temporal, and geographical shifts within shared support.

</details>


### [114] [SITA: Semantic Interest Tokens for Target-Aware Compression in Long-Sequence Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03692)
*Rui Zhou,Bo Chen,Qinglin Jia,Jiezhou Ji,Chaoyi Ma,Ruiming Tang,Hao Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SITA框架，通过语义量化学习语义标识符，将压缩的用户兴趣组织成语义结构，实现既保持压缩表示的高效可扩展性，又具备目标感知的自适应建模能力，在公开和工业数据集上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长序列推荐方法存在两难困境：动态检索方法支持目标感知但推理时依赖目标计算，压缩表示方法高效可扩展但缺乏目标特定适应性。关键挑战是如何在保持压缩表示效率的同时实现目标感知建模。

Method: SITA框架通过并行语义量化学习语义标识符，将压缩的兴趣组织成语义结构；基于目标物品的语义标识符，自适应聚合相应的结构化兴趣以构建目标特定的用户表示。

Result: 在公开数据集和大规模工业数据集上的大量实验表明，SITA在保持强可扩展性的同时，一致性地优于代表性基线方法，展现出在真实推荐系统中的强大潜力。

Conclusion: SITA成功弥合了目标感知建模与压缩表示效率之间的鸿沟，为长序列推荐提供了一个兼顾性能与可扩展性的有效解决方案，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: As user behavior histories continue to grow on modern Internet platforms, effectively modeling long behavior sequences has become crucial for predicting user interests in candidate items. Existing methods have evolved along two directions. One line dynamically retrieves target-relevant behaviors from long histories, enabling target-aware modeling but requiring target-dependent computation during inference. The other line compresses entire behavior sequences into compact user representations, achieving high efficiency and scalability but sacrificing target-specific adaptation due to target-independent encoding. The key challenge is therefore to enable target-aware modeling while preserving the efficiency and scalability of compressed user representations. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{SITA}, a target-aware compression framework for long-sequence recommendation. SITA enables target-aware compression by organizing compressed interests into semantic structures through semantic identifiers learned via parallel semantic quantization. Conditioned on the semantic identifier of the target item, SITA adaptively aggregates the corresponding structured interests to construct the target-specific user representation. Extensive experiments on public datasets and a large-scale industrial dataset demonstrate that SITA consistently outperforms representative baselines while maintaining strong scalability, highlighting its strong potential for real-world recommender systems.

</details>


### [115] [LegalPincite: Multi-level Legal Information Retrieval Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03756)
*Theresia Veronika Rampisela,Henrik Palmer Olsen,Giovanni Colavizza*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个大规模的欧洲法院（CJEU）法律检索数据集，该数据集包含去除引用信息的掩码查询、包含全部段落的语料库以及案件级和段落级的人工验证标注，支持多种检索粒度的法律信息检索任务开发与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律IR数据集存在两个主要问题：一是在查询文本中包含数据泄露（引用信息直接作为查询），二是语料库中排除了既非引用也非被引的段落，导致检索设置不切实际且过于简化，可能造成性能虚高。同时大多数数据集缺乏段落级引用标注。

Method: 从欧洲法院（CJEU）判决中构建大规模法律IR数据集，具体包括：(i) 掩码化的案件/段落查询（移除引用信息）；(ii) 包含所有段落的完整语料库；(iii) 案件级和段落级的地面真值引用标注，并进行了部分人类专家验证。数据集支持案件到案件、段落到案件、段落到段落三种检索粒度。

Result: 构建了一个包含掩码查询、完整语料库和多重粒度标注的大规模法律检索数据集，并通过部分人工专家验证保证标注质量，公开提供下载链接。

Conclusion: 该数据集弥补了现有法律IR数据集在段落级引用标注、数据泄露控制和语料库完整性方面的不足，为法律信息检索方法的开发和严格评估提供了更现实和全面的基准。

Abstract: A common task in legal Information Retrieval (IR) is to find relevant legal sources from case-law collections. While legal practice often requires pinpoint citations (pincites) to specific case paragraphs, most existing public legal IR datasets lack paragraph-level citation annotations. Yet, publicly available datasets with such information contain data leakage in the query text and exclude paragraphs that are neither citing nor cited from the corpora, creating an unrealistic and oversimplified retrieval setting, potentially leading to inflated performance. To address these limitations, we contribute a large-scale legal IR dataset constructed from Court of Justice of the European Union (CJEU) judgments. The dataset contains: (i) masked case/paragraph queries, with removed citation information; (ii) a corpus that includes all paragraphs; and (iii) case- and paragraph-level ground-truth citations, with partial human expert validation. Our dataset supports both the development and rigorous evaluation of legal IR methods, at multiple query-document levels (case-to-case, paragraph-to-case, and paragraph-to-paragraph retrieval). Link to dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/theresiavr/legalpincite

</details>


### [116] [ATLAS: Learning to Recommend Across Unseen Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03899)
*Pervez Shaik,Prosenjit Biswas,Abhinav Thorat,Ravi Kolla,Niranjan Pedanekar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ATLAS框架，通过多源推荐域泛化实现零样本推荐——在多个异构源域上学习域不变的用户-物品表示，无需目标域适配或语言模型预训练，即可直接推广到完全未见过的领域。在五个亚马逊源域训练并应用于十个未见域，平均HitRate相对提升24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统具有领域绑定特性，一个领域训练的模型难以直接部署到新目录（如电影推荐器无法直接推荐杂货或游戏）。现有方法要么迁移受限的推荐知识，要么需要目标域适配，要么依赖大语言模型。作者希望探索能否仅从多个异构领域学习通用的推荐知识，实现真正零样本的跨域推荐。

Method: ATLAS框架包含三个核心组件：(1) Gromov-Wasserstein对齐，保持用户跨域间相互关系；(2) 对抗性目标，使物品表示跨域不可区分；(3) 残差向量量化（RVQ）码本，将用户和物品嵌入压缩为离散潜空间，捕获层次化交互模式并抑制领域特定变异。

Result: 在五个亚马逊源域训练后直接应用于十个未见目标域，ATLAS在大多数未见域上优于SOTA的序列、图、跨域、量化和基于LLM的基线，平均HitRate相对提升24%。消融实验验证了各组件的有效性，并发现源域多样性效应显著——增加源域异构性可大幅提升零样本迁移性能。

Conclusion: ATLAS验证了推荐域泛化作为零样本推荐的新兴范式的可行性，证明了从多个异构领域学习的推荐知识可以泛化到完全未见领域，无需目标域适配或LLM预训练。

Abstract: Recommender systems remain domain-bound: a model trained on one interaction environment typically requires retraining or target-domain adaptation before it can operate on a new catalogue. A recommender trained on movies cannot be directly deployed to recommend groceries or video games. Existing approaches mitigate this by transferring restricted forms of recommendation knowledge, adapting to the target domain, or leveraging large language models (LLMs) for transferable representations. We instead ask whether recommendation-specific knowledge learned solely from multiple heterogeneous domains can generalize to entirely unseen domains without target-domain adaptation or language-model pretraining. We introduce ATLAS, a multi-source recommendation domain generalization framework that learns a shared, domain-invariant user-item representation from disjoint source domains, enabling zero-shot recommendation on unseen domains. ATLAS combines a Gromov-Wasserstein alignment that preserves how users relate to one another across domains, an adversarial objective that makes item representations indistinguishable across domains, and residual vector quantization (RVQ) codebooks that compress user and item embeddings into a discrete latent space, capturing hierarchical interaction patterns while suppressing domain-specific variation. Trained on five Amazon domains and applied directly to ten unseen domains, ATLAS outperforms state-of-the-art sequential, graph-based, cross-domain, quantization-based, and LLM-based baselines on most unseen domains, with an average relative gain in HitRate of 24%. Ablations and representation analyses validate each component, and we identify a pronounced source-domain diversity effect: increasing source heterogeneity substantially improves zero-shot transfer. ATLAS establishes recommendation domain generalization as a promising paradigm for zero-shot recommendation.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [An Empirical Analysis of the Glob Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02610)
*Phyllis Lim,Connor Adkins,Mark Marron*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对glob模式（一种用于结构化字符串匹配的领域特定语言）生态系统进行了实证研究，通过分析1,966个开源项目、1,355个GitHub问题、444个CVE报告和361个StackOverflow帖子，系统性地揭示了glob在不同编程语言和工具间的一致性问题、安全漏洞和可用性缺陷，并提出了一个名为GlobSpec的形式化规范来解决这些问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: glob模式虽然被广泛用于构建脚本、配置文件、Web服务器路由等现代软件开发场景中，但缺乏标准化导致生态系统碎片化，存在行为不一致、安全漏洞和可用性陷阱等问题。此前缺乏对这些问题进行系统性量化的实证研究。

Method: 对1,966个开源项目、1,355个GitHub问题、444个CVE报告和361个StackOverflow帖子进行大规模实证分析，跨六个常见的glob生态系统系统性地映射功能支持和实际使用情况，并基于分析结果提出GlobSpec形式化规范。

Result: 研究发现各个glob实现与开发者采用之间存在显著差异，文档记录了一致性问题如何阻碍可移植性和可靠性并造成安全缺陷。安全漏洞并非边缘情况，而是主导性问题，约占所有开发者讨论的四分之一。

Conclusion: 提出通过标准化解决问题的路径，引入形式化规范GlobSpec，旨在解决语义歧义并弥合当前实现与开发者需求之间的表达力差距。

Abstract: Glob patterns, a domain-specific language for structured string matching, are a foundational yet understudied component of modern software development embedded in everything from build scripts and configuration files to web server routes. However, this widespread use rests on a fragile foundation. Despite their ubiquity across tools and programming languages, a lack of standardization has led to a fragmented ecosystem rife with inconsistent behaviors, security vulnerabilities, and usability pitfalls. This paper presents the first empirical study of the glob ecosystem to quantify these challenges and chart a path toward robust solutions.
  Through an analysis of 1,966 open source projects, 1,355 Github issues, 444 CVE reports, and 361 StackOverflow posts, we systematically map the feature support and real-world usage of globs across six common ecosystems that utilize globs. Our findings reveal a stark divide between various globbing implementations and developer adoption. In addition, we document inconsistencies that hinder portability, reliability, and create security flaws. Our analysis reveals that security vulnerabilities are not corner cases, but a dominant concern, comprising almost a quarter of all developer discussions. We propose a path forward through standardization and introduce a formal specification for globs, GlobSpec, designed to resolve semantic ambiguities and bridge the expressiveness gap between current implementations and developer requirements.

</details>


### [118] [Rethinking Self-Evolving Agent Skills: Feedback Dynamics over Multiple Rounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02636)
*Yuxuan Liu,Zhaochen Su,Yuhao Zhang,Jiahe Guo,Zhongwei Xie,Huihao Jing,Lingyun Xie,Qing Zong,Yauwai Yim,Zhixiong Zhang,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个受控评估框架，系统研究了智能体自我进化技能系统的有效性。通过在5个基准、3个模型上的42次反馈实验，发现技能进化是稀疏的（仅55/388个候选产生新的验证最优），且依赖于包含失败轨迹的反馈。与测试时计算扩展相比，持久化技能进化并非总能带来额外收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然自我进化技能系统声称能通过执行反馈持久更新技能来提升智能体性能，但现有研究缺乏对"何时进化有益"、"成功与失败轨迹如何影响修订"及"额外的测试时计算能否替代进化收益"等关键问题的系统理解。

Method: 构建受控评估框架，在14个模型-基准设置上固定执行器、优化器配置、修订流程、验证规则和轮次预算，仅改变反馈类型（成功+失败、仅失败、仅成功）。通过42次反馈运行和广泛的SearchQA分析（8个模型），并设置GPT-5.5测试时扩展对照（并行采样与顺序精炼）进行比较。

Result: 进化是稀疏的，388个候选中仅55个产生字节级不同的验证最优；14个设置中有11个选择了进化技能，其中9个提升了测试性能；所有11个选择都来自包含失败轨迹的反馈条件；测试时扩展对照中，并行采样在SearchQA上仅差0.43分但在SpreadsheetBench上仍落后30.96分，顺序精炼无法恢复任何增益。

Conclusion: 持久化技能自我进化应被理解为稀疏的、经验证过滤的搜索过程，其收益依赖于模型和基准，而非从额外轮次中持续改进的结果。验证集与下游评估（测试、鲁棒性、迁移）有时会偏好不同的反馈视角。

Abstract: Self-evolving skill systems promise to improve agents by turning execution feedback into persistent skill updates without changing the underlying model. Yet it remains unclear when further evolution helps, how successful and failed trajectories shape revision, and whether extra test-time computation can recover the same gains. To address these questions, we present a controlled evaluation framework across five benchmarks and three models. Our primary study contains 42 feedback runs across 14 supported model-benchmark settings. Within each setting, we hold the executor and optimizer configuration, revision procedure, validation rule, and round budget fixed, while varying only the feedback shown to the optimizer: successes and failures (Normal), failures only, or successes only. Evolution is sparse: only 55 of 388 candidates establish byte-distinct validation bests. Validation-based selection chooses an evolved skill in 11 of 14 settings, nine of which improve released-test performance. All 11 selections come from feedback conditions that include failed trajectories, although the relative ranking of Normal and Fail-only varies across settings. Validation and downstream evaluations on test, robustness, and transfer sometimes favor different feedback views. A broader SearchQA analysis covering eight models shows similarly sparse, feedback-dependent dynamics. In the GPT-5.5 test-time-scaling controls, oracle Parallel Sampling comes within 0.43 points of the evolved SearchQA skill but remains 30.96 points behind on SpreadsheetBench; Sequential Refinement recovers neither gain. Overall, persistent skill self-evolution is better understood as sparse, validation-filtered search with model- and benchmark-dependent returns, rather than steady improvement from additional rounds. The implementation is available at https://github.com/HKUST-KnowComp/rethinkskill.

</details>


### [119] [Studying, Identifying, and Fixing Hidden Technical Debt in AI-Intensive Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02638)
*Beena*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文旨在刻画AI-CPS（人工智能网络物理系统）中的技术债务特征，并提出识别和修复这些技术债务的方法，最终开发自动化工具支持AI解决方案来监控、治理和偿还这些债务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI组件在包括CPS在内的软件系统中日益普及，而AI-CPS由硬件、AI组件和传统模块组成，其技术债务独特且比传统系统更具挑战性，但目前缺乏对其系统性的刻画和修复方法。

Method: 研究分三个阶段：首先通过分析AI生态系统和AI-CPS代码仓库，并访谈开发者来刻画AI-CPS技术债务的特征；然后基于获取的知识定义识别和缓解技术债务的方法；最后开发和验证支持智能体AI解决方案的自动化工具，用于监控、治理和偿还AI-CPS技术债务。

Result: 研究尚处于规划阶段，预期成果包括：对AI-CPS技术债务的系统性刻画，有效的识别与修复方法，以及一个支持智能体AI的自动化工具。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一套系统性的研究框架，从技术债务的刻画、识别与缓解，到自动化工具的开发与验证，旨在全面解决AI-CPS中的技术债务问题。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) components are increasingly pervasive in several software systems, including Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs). AI-CPS are used in several domains, including autonomous vehicles, industry, home automation, robotics, and healthcare. Being composed of hardware, AI components, and conventional modules, AI-CPS can exhibit technical debt (TD) that is peculiar and potentially more challenging than that of conventional systems. This thesis aims to characterize AI-CPS TD and propose approaches for its identification and repair. In a first phase, we characterize AI-CPS TD by analyzing AI ecosystems and AI-CPS repositories, as well as interviewing developers. Based on the acquired knowledge, we define approaches to identify and mitigate such TD. Finally, we plan to develop and validate an automated tool that supports agentic AI solutions to monitor, govern, and repay AI-CPS TD.

</details>


### [120] [Instruction Stacking Collapse: A Benchmark and the Capability-Dependent Value of Prompt Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02639)
*Atul Anand,Sourav Chattaraj*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究多指令叠加条件下指令遵循能力的退化问题，构建了包含24条可验证指令的基准测试，发现模型遵循率从96%非线性下降至20%，并提出一种免训练的指令编译器方法，对较弱模型可恢复最多11个百分点的遵循率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中系统提示通常同时包含多条指令（如JSON格式、字数限制、引用数量、语气要求等），但现有研究缺乏对多指令叠加条件下模型遵循能力退化的系统性理解。

Method: 构建一个可叠加24条可验证指令的基准测试（每次叠加1到20条），评估三类生产级LLM（Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT-5-mini、Gemini 2.5 Flash）；提出免训练的指令编译器，通过单次LLM调用重写叠加提示并在多次查询间复用；采用聚类稳健检验、同基线对照和族内规模阶梯实验进行归因分析。

Result: 指令遵循率随约束累积呈非线性下降（从约96%降至最低20%），且由结构化、可复现的成对冲突驱动，例如单一"输出JSON"约束与另外9条约束不可同时满足；指令编译器对较弱模型的遵循率恢复最多提升11个百分点，对较强模型几乎无变化。

Conclusion: 指令编译器作为免训练补救方案的能力增益是分级（capability-graded）的：对规模化部署中更常见的较弱模型效果显著，且增益归因于重写本身而非额外token、重排或测量余量。

Abstract: Production prompts rarely carry a single instruction. One system message may require valid JSON, a word limit, three citations, and a fixed tone at the same time. We study how instruction-following degrades as such constraints accumulate. We introduce a benchmark that stacks 24 verifier-checked instructions, one to twenty at a time, and evaluate three production-tier LLMs (Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5-mini, Gemini 2.5 Flash). Instruction-following degrades non-linearly: the follow rate falls from ~96% to as low as 20%, driven by a structured and reproducible set of pairwise conflicts. A single "output JSON" constraint, for example, is jointly unsatisfiable with nine others. We then evaluate a training-free remedy: an instruction compiler that rewrites the stacked prompt in a single LLM call and is reused across queries. Its benefit is capability-graded. It recovers up to +11 points of follow rate for weaker models, which are also the models most often deployed at scale, while leaving stronger models, which already internalise the same structure, essentially unchanged. Cluster-robust tests, same-baseline controls, and a within-family scaling ladder attribute the gain to the rewrite itself rather than to additional tokens, reordering, or measurement headroom. We release the benchmark, verifiers, and cached runs for full reproduction.

</details>


### [121] [IR2Solve: Structured Intermediate Representations for Cost-Efficient Optimization Autoformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02641)
*Penglin Zhu,Linhai Zhang,Jungang Xu,Xinchi Wei,Xiuqi Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出IR2Solve，一种以中间表示（ModelIR）为核心的优化问题自动建模管道。它通过一次语义LLM调用生成模式约束的ModelIR，再经过确定性验证和IR到求解器编译两个阶段，在保证目标正确性的同时大幅降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接让LLM生成求解器代码存在脆弱性问题：模式、索引和语义错误会导致编译失败、模型不可行或目标错误；而迭代修复、搜索和多智能体工作流虽然能改善但会显著增加推理成本。

Method: IR2Solve采用三步管道：（1）单一语义LLM调用生成模式约束的ModelIR（显式表示集合、参数、变量、目标和约束，使用受限Python风格表达式）；（2）确定性验证阶段；（3）IR到求解器的确定性编译。其中"标量约束约定"将有限逐索引约束族表示为独立条目，减少自由索引和隐式量化错误。

Result: 在六个优化基准上取得强目标正确性，与近期优化建模系统相当。消融实验显示结构化IR接口、标量约束指令和确定性验证均带来递增收益。成本对比中，IR2Solve每实例仅1次语义调用，而Chain-of-Experts和SAC-Opt分别为8次和39次，token消耗仅为后者的3.3倍和22.9倍更低的水平。

Conclusion: 结构化中间表示结合确定性后处理能够在LLM驱动的优化自动建模中实现实用的精度-成本权衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can translate natural-language optimization problems into solver-ready formulations, but direct code generation is brittle: schema, indexing, and semantic errors can cause compilation failures, infeasible models, or incorrect objectives, while iterative repair, search, and multi-agent workflows increase inference cost. We present IR2Solve, an intermediate-representation-first autoformulation pipeline that uses a single semantic LLM call to produce a schema-constrained ModelIR, followed by two deterministic stages: verification and IR-to-solver compilation. ModelIR explicitly represents sets, parameters, variables, objectives, and constraints using restricted Python-like expression strings. A concrete scalar-constraint convention represents finite per-index constraint families as individual entries, reducing free-index and implicit-quantification errors while simplifying downstream verification and compilation. Across six cleaned optimization benchmarks, IR2Solve achieves strong objective correctness and remains competitive with recent optimization-modeling systems. A controlled ablation on 153 IndustryOR and ComplexLP instances shows sequential gains from the structured IR interface, the scalar-constraint instruction, and deterministic verification. On a matched ten-instance cost panel, IR2Solve uses one semantic call per instance, whereas Chain-of-Experts and SAC-Opt use 8 and 39 calls per instance and consume 3.3 and 22.9 times the token volume of IR2Solve, respectively. These results show that structured intermediate representations, combined with deterministic post-generation processing, provide a practical accuracy-cost trade-off for LLM-based optimization autoformulation.

</details>


### [122] [CUADebug: Diagnosing and Repairing Computer-Use Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02643)
*Weijia Zhang,Kunlun Zhu,Zeyi Liu,Yinting Chen,Tianyi Ma,Jiateng Liu,Jiaxun Zhang,Bingxuan Li,Xiangru Tang,Heng Ji,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CUADebug框架，用于诊断和修复计算机使用智能体（CUA）的失败。包含错误分类体系、人类标注的错误基准（CUAErrorBench）和工具增强的调试器（CUADebugger）。实验表明该方法能将根因诊断准确率从11.2%提升至19.6%，并显著提高任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CUA在真实桌面和网页界面中操作时常失败，且失败原因涉及视觉感知、空间定位、低层交互、任务推理和环境动态等多方面耦合，难以诊断和修复。现有方法缺乏针对CUA失败的专门诊断框架。

Method: 提出CUADebug框架：(1) 定义CUA特定错误分类体系；(2) 构建人类标注的OSWorld失败基准CUAErrorBench（204条失败轨迹）；(3) 开发CUADebugger调试器，主动检查可疑步骤的前后截图和操作轨迹，输出结构化诊断（根因步骤、错误类型、证据、修复策略）并重新执行。

Result: 204条失败轨迹中，任务推理与控制是最大失败类别（110/204），其后依次为感知（36）、定位/交互（25）、外部/系统（13）等。在Claude智能体上，联合子类型和步骤诊断从11.2%提升至19.6%；单次重执行中，根因分析方法任务完成率（28.47%-29.90%）显著优于单纯历史延续（13.89%）；持续重执行中成功率从12.2%提升至25.86%（人类标注引导可达29.21%）。

Conclusion: CUA根因诊断能够提供可操作的修复信号，而不仅仅是事后解释，证明结构化诊断方法对CUA失败修复具有实际价值。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) operate real desktop and web interfaces through screenshots, mouse and keyboard actions, and stateful UI feedback, yet their failures remain difficult to diagnose and repair. Unlike text-only agents, CUA failures arise from coupled visual perception, spatial grounding, low-level interaction, task reasoning, and environment dynamics, making debugging a distinctive multimodal causal localization problem. We introduce CUADebug, a framework for diagnosing and repairing CUA failures. CUADebug includes a CUA-specific error taxonomy, CUAErrorBench, a human-annotated OSWorld failure benchmark, and CUADebugger, a tool-augmented debugger. Instead of prompting over the full trajectory once, CUADebugger actively inspects suspicious steps with paired before/after screenshots and action traces, then submits a structured diagnosis containing the root-cause step, error type, grounded evidence, and corrective strategy for re-execution. Human annotations over 204 failed trajectories show that task reasoning and control is the largest failure family (110/204), followed by perception (36), grounding/interaction (25), external/system (13), and an others category of 20 OSWorld infeasible-task cases. On the main Claude-agent split, CUADebugger improves joint subtype-and-step diagnosis from 11.2% to 19.6% with Gemini 2.5 Pro and improves consistently across debugger backbones. In single re-execution package evaluation, RCA-based conditions achieve higher task completion than history-only continuation (28.47% with machine RCA and 29.90% with our method, versus 13.89%); in continual re-execution, our method improves success from 12.2% to 25.86%, while human-oracle guidance reaches 29.21%. These results show that CUA root-cause diagnosis can provide actionable repair signals rather than merely post-hoc explanations.

</details>


### [123] [A Unified Feature Model for Microservice Identification and Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02644)
*Ana Almeida,António Rito Silva*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于微服务识别工具的特征模型，基于对现有技术的广泛分析，并通过系统文献映射和现有工具架构实例化来评估该模型。研究发现该特征模型能捕捉现有方法的主要变体点，但现有工具均只覆盖模型的一小部分，且彼此之间缺乏整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前微服务自动识别方法多样，但在数据收集、分析技术、分解算法和可视化机制等方面缺乏统一的比较框架，且没有单一最优方法，需要整合多种方法以充分探索设计方案的权衡。

Method: 通过对现有研究进行广泛分析提出特征模型，并通过两方面的评估来验证：一是对代表性文献进行系统性映射，二是在现有微服务识别工具架构中分析该模型的实例化情况。

Result: 特征模型成功捕捉了现有方法的主要变体点，为分析、比较和设计微服务识别工具提供了统一基础；虽然没有任何单独工具覆盖模型的大部分，但所分析的工具共同覆盖了几乎所有变体点。

Conclusion: 设计空间已被填充但碎片化——各种方案分散在独立的工具中，没有任何工具能够比较或整合其他工具所实现的替代方案，这凸显了构建统一框架以整合互补方法的必要性。

Abstract: Several approaches have been proposed for the automatic identification of microservices within monolithic systems. These methodologies differ in their data collection and analysis techniques, the decomposition algorithms applied, and the mechanisms used to visualize and refine candidate microservices. Despite this diversity, systematic experimentation and comparison across approaches remain limited by the absence of a common conceptual framework. Furthermore, current research indicates that no single optimal method exists; rather, integrating multiple approaches is necessary to fully explore the trade-offs inherent in any design solution.
  To address this gap, this paper proposes a feature model for variant-rich microservice identification tools, grounded in an extensive analysis of the state of the art. We evaluate this feature model through a systematic mapping of representative literature and by analyzing its instantiation within the architecture of an existing microservice identification tool. Our findings are two-fold. First, the proposed feature model successfully captures the primary variation points of existing methodologies, providing a unifying foundation for analyzing, comparing, and designing microservice identification tools. Second, while no individual tool covers more than a fraction of the model, the analyzed tools jointly span almost all of its variation points. This complementarity is the central result: the design space is already populated, but it is fragmented across standalone tools, none of which can compare or integrate the alternatives that the others implement.

</details>


### [124] [Verified Tool Calls Improve LLM Agent Reliability Under Non-Atomic Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02645)
*Isham Kalappurackal Mansoor,Abhishek Phadke,Pratip Rana*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级的、支持验证的工具包装器方法，用于增强LLM代理与外部工具的交互可靠性，通过后置条件验证、验证-再重试逻辑和幂等键处理非原子性工具调用问题，在模拟环境中显著减少重复操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理框架假设工具调用是原子性的并返回二元成功/失败信号，但真实系统存在超时、延迟可见性和部分状态更新等非原子行为，导致重复操作、不必要的工具执行等可靠性问题。

Method: 引入一种轻量级、验证感知的工具包装器（wrapper），通过三种机制增强工具调用：(1) 后置条件验证（postcondition verification）、(2) 验证-再重试逻辑（verify-before-retry）、(3) 幂等键（idempotency keys）。在受控模拟环境中注入非原子性故障进行多任务模板评估。

Result: 在模拟环境中，该方法显著减少了重复操作（duplicate actions），同时保持了可比的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 强化工具交互语义是提升LLM代理可靠性且有前景的方向，无需修改底层语言模型即可实现可靠性改进。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents rely on external tools to perform multistage tasks. Existing agent frameworks typically assume that tool calls are atomic and return binary success or failure signals. However, real-world systems exhibit non-atomic behaviors such as timeouts after dispatch, delayed visibility, and partial state updates. These mismatches lead to reliability issues including duplicate actions, task success, and unnecessary tool executions. A lightweight, verification-aware tool wrapper is introduced that augments tool calls with postcondition verification, verify-before-retry logic, and idempotency keys. The approach is evaluated in a controlled simulated environment with injected non-atomic failures across multiple task templates. The results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly reduces duplicate actions, while maintaining comparable task success rates. Overall, the findings suggest that strengthening tool interaction semantics is a promising direction for improving LLM agent reliability without requiring modifications to the underlying language model.

</details>


### [125] [Intent-Level Quantum Programming with Assertion-Guided Execution and Inspectable Intermediate Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02648)
*Ilesh Vora,Srikanth Thudumu,John Carlson,Harshil Kamdar,Rajesh Vasa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量子领域特定语言（QDSL），通过意图-执行分离、一等IR内省和断言引导的模态推断三个机制，解决量子程序难以验证、可移植性差的问题，并在基准电路上验证了其故障检测和跨后端一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子程序验证面临多重挑战：电路通常以命令式门序列表达、难以检查内部结构；执行模态需手动选择；输出本质上是概率性的。此外，程序需要跨不同后端框架移植，而各框架的内部约定不一致，这些因素共同使得量子程序的开发和验证变得困难。

Method: 该QDSL通过三个机制解决问题：(i) 意图-执行分离，将制备、叠加、纠缠、测量等算法构造表示为可检查对象，在预执行IR验证后才编译为后端特定电路；(ii) 一等IR内省，在执行前暴露电路宽度、线映射、操作顺序和测量意图供开发者检查；(iii) 断言引导的模态推断，引擎根据声明的验证属性自动选择采样、状态向量或双重执行模态。结果以结构化形式记录以支持可复现的回归测试。

Result: 在纠缠、基于Oracle、结构化变换和变分示例的基准电路上评估原型。在Bell和3-qubit GHZ电路的故障注入实验中，全变差距离检测器在所有shot预算下均识别出注入的故障（真正例率1.0），仅在128 shots时观察到2.7-3.8%的假阳性率。差分测试表明在endianness规范化后，PennyLane和Qiskit编译目标在数值上一致。IR生成成本低于毫秒级。

Conclusion: 该QDSL通过意图-执行分离、IR内省和自动模态推断，有效地提高了量子程序的可验证性和跨后端可移植性，在故障检测、差分一致性和编译效率方面均表现出良好性能，为量子程序的可靠开发提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Quantum programs are difficult to validate: circuits are typically expressed as imperative gate sequences with limited inspectability, execution modalities must be selected manually, and outputs are inherently probabilistic. These challenges are compounded when programs must be portable across backend frameworks with differing internal conventions. We present a quantum domain-specific language (QDSL) that addresses these problems through three mechanisms: (i) intent-execution separation, where algorithmic constructs such as preparation, superposition, entanglement, and measurement are represented as inspectable objects compiled into backend-specific circuits only after pre-execution Intermediate Representation (IR) validation; (ii) first-class IR introspection, exposing circuit width, wire mapping, operation order, and measurement intent for developer inspection prior to execution; and (iii) assertion-guided modality inference, where the engine examines declared verification properties to automatically select sampling, statevector, or dual execution without user intervention. Results are logged in structured form to support reproducible regression testing. We evaluate our prototype on benchmark circuits spanning entanglement, oracle-based, structured-transform, and variational examples. In fault-injection experiments on Bell and 3-qubit GHZ circuits, a total-variation-distance detector identifies the injected faults across all evaluated shot budgets (True Positive Rate 1.0), with observed false positives (False Positive Rate 2.7-3.8%) only at 128 shots. Differential testing demonstrates numerical agreement across PennyLane and Qiskit compilation targets for the evaluated constructs after endianness canonicalization. IR generation costs are sub-millisecond.

</details>


### [126] [When Policies Change Probabilities: Modular Decision-Making for LLM Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02677)
*Rasvik Kudum,Max Corbett,Hitansh Paliwal,Romaisa Fatima,Thomas Jiralerspong,Sneheel Sarangi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM代码审查器在风险评估与决策（是否批准补丁）之间是否保持了分离性。通过15,792个响应（涵盖720个候选补丁）对四种部署的审查界面进行测试，发现将等成本策略替换为10:1假接受策略会导致报告的失败概率平均变化13.6至16.9个百分点，且所有审查器在高成本提示下的行为比全部拒绝更差。相比之下，一个将风险评估、外部监控与成本决策分离的模块化流水线在概率准确性和平均损失方面均有改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代码审查器通常在单个提示中同时估计补丁风险并做出审批决策，但概率估计应基于证据，决策应基于成本。这种混杂可能导致下游策略影响模型报告的概率本身，从而损害决策质量。

Method: 使用15,792个来自四个部署审查界面的响应，对360个仓库问题（每个问题有一个通过存档测试的补丁和一个失败的补丁，共720个候选补丁）进行匹配实验。在证据固定不变的条件下，改变策略成本提示（等成本vs 10:1假接受），比较报告的失败概率和返回的行动。同时构建一个模块化流水线：独立的风险评估（不含策略信息）、独立监控分数、以及在代码中应用成本决策。

Result: 将等成本策略替换为10:1假接受策略时，报告的失败概率平均变化13.6至16.9个百分点；所有审查器在高成本提示下返回的行动均劣于全部拒绝。对等成本下获得的概率应用高成本规则可以降低所有四种系统的损失。模块化流水线相比校准的审查器分数提高了平均概率准确性，在等成本下平均损失降低0.073/问题，接受58-68%的补丁；在10:1成本下不接受任何补丁并匹配全部拒绝的表现。

Conclusion: 下游决策策略可以改变模型报告的概率本身，这表明单独评估风险估计、外部证据与行动决策的重要性。模块化设计将概率估计与成本决策分离，能够更准确地评估风险并做出更优的决策。

Abstract: LLM code reviewers often estimate patch risk and make approval decisions in one prompt. A probability should depend on evidence; costs should determine the action taken from it. We test whether four deployed reviewer interfaces preserve this separation using 15,792 responses on 720 candidate patches, with one that passed and one that failed an archived test harness for each of 360 repository issues. In matched calls with the patch and monitor evidence fixed, replacing an equal-cost policy with a 10:1 false-accept policy changes reported failure probabilities by 13.6 to 16.9 percentage points on average. For every reviewer, the actions returned under the high-cost prompt are worse than rejecting all patches. Applying the same high-cost rule to probabilities elicited under equal costs reduces loss for all four systems, showing that probability elicitation itself contributes to the excess loss. We also evaluate a modular pipeline that elicits risk without policy information, combines an independent monitor score, and applies costs in code. Relative to calibrated reviewer-only scores, the pipeline improves average probability accuracy and, at equal costs, reduces mean loss by .073 per issue while accepting 58 to 68% of patches. At 10:1, it accepts none and matches reject-all. Downstream policy can therefore change the probability it is meant to use, motivating separate evaluation of risk, outside evidence, and action.

</details>


### [127] [AI Sandbox: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02679)
*Muhammad Waseem,Md Aidul Islam,Md Nasir Uddin Shuvo,Md Mahade Hasan,Kai-Kristian Kemell,Jussi Rasku,Mika Saari,Vilma Saari,Roope Pajasmaa,Markku Oivo,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个治理感知的多租户AI沙箱平台的设计与实现，该平台支持跨行业与学术界合作的AI实验，同时兼顾访问控制、租户隔离和透明的治理流程，并生成可复用的评估证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI沙箱日益受到关注，但关于如何设计和实现既能支持快速实验又能满足治理要求的平台，缺乏实用的指导。

Method: 在行业-学术界合作背景下，基于与工业伙伴迭代细化的需求，设计了参考架构，将多租户用户界面与后端控制平面分离，并在专门层次中放置执行和数据管理功能；平台支持治理导向的用户接入、项目中心化协作、AI服务受控访问、审批流程、审计日志和可追踪实验，实验配置与治理决策存储为持久记录。

Result: 开发过程提供了在协作研究和工业环境中部署和扩展治理感知AI沙箱平台的实用经验和教训。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了AI沙箱设计与实施层面的实践指导空白，为跨机构协作中的受控实验和证据复用提供了可行的平台方案。

Abstract: Collaborative AI experimentation across industry and academia requires platforms that enable rapid prototyping while preserving controlled access, tenant separation, and transparent workflows. Despite growing interest in AI sandboxes, there is still limited practical guidance on how to design and implement platforms that integrate experimentation capabilities with governance requirements. This work presents the design and implementation of a governance-aware, multi-tenant AI sandbox for structured experimentation and the generation of reusable evaluation evidence across projects and stakeholder groups.
  The sandbox was developed within an industry-academia collaboration based on requirements that were iteratively refined with industrial partners. Its reference architecture separates the multi-tenant user interface from the backend control plane and places execution and data-management functions in dedicated layers. The platform supports governed user onboarding, project-centered collaboration, managed access to AI services, approval workflows, audit logging, and traceable experimentation. Experiment configurations, contextual information, and governance decisions are stored as persistent records, allowing evidence and outcomes to be compared and reused across projects.
  The development process provides practical lessons for deploying and extending governance-aware AI sandbox platforms in collaborative research and industrial environments.

</details>


### [128] [TraceCompiler: Skill-Guided Mining and Compilation of LLM Agent Traces into Mostly Deterministic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02680)
*Salma El Yadouni,Guanyi Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TraceCompiler，一种基于技能引导的系统，能从嘈杂的LLM代理轨迹聚类中挖掘并编译出可执行、基本确定性的工作流。它通过严格的值唯一性归因来建立工具间依赖关系，仅接受可审计的硬边，并将绑定分类为常量、用户输入、复制输出、变换或残余LLM决策。实验显示依赖恢复精度达0.928/0.943，在AppWorld上精度达0.993，编译Venmo意图时调用数从34降至11。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在执行任务时会反复重新发现已执行过的程序，产生的轨迹混合了可复用结构与重试、探索、意外排序和重复查找等噪声。现有方法无法有效从这些嘈杂轨迹中提取可复用的确定性工作流，因此需要一个系统能够自动化地挖掘并编译这些轨迹成为可执行的工作流。

Method: TraceCompiler采用技能引导的方式，对噪声代理轨迹聚类后进行编译。核心规则是：仅当消费方参数包含可唯一归因于先前生产者的值时，才承认工具间依赖关系（硬边）；每个硬边携带可审计的证据元组；模糊关系标记为疑似且不施加排序约束。绑定被分类为常量、用户输入、复制输出、变换或残余LLM决策五类。

Result: 在T1上，依赖恢复的精确率和召回率分别为0.928和0.943（15,775条def-use边），优于邻接方法的0.711 F1和频率阈值直接跟随法的0.712；技能运行器在250条边达到0.992。在AppWorld上，确定性模拟器回放后对563条token边达到0.993精确率。Venmo意图编译将34次API调用降至11次，通过15/21状态测试；Spotify/Todoit意图因不可逆副作用不确定而被正确拒绝编译。

Conclusion: TraceCompiler展示了从嘈杂代理轨迹中系统化提取可复用工作流的可行性，通过严格的可审计依赖识别和绑定分类，能生成确定性更高的编译工作流，并能正确识别不应编译的场景。作者承认只测量了调用减少而未测量离线编译成本，因此不声称净效率提升。

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents repeatedly rediscover procedures they have already executed, producing traces that mix reusable structure with retries, exploration, accidental ordering, and repeated lookups. We present TraceCompiler, a skill-guided system that mines clusters of noisy agent traces and compiles them into executable, mostly deterministic workflows. It admits an inter-tool dependency only when a consumer argument contains a value attributable uniquely to an earlier producer; every hard edge carries an auditable evidence tuple, and ambiguous relations are marked suspected and impose no ordering constraint. Bindings are classified as constants, user inputs, copied outputs, transforms, or residual LLM decisions. On T1, a mechanized form of the rule recovers producer-consumer dependencies at 0.928 precision and 0.943 recall over 15,775 def-use edges of its training split, against 0.711 F1 for adjacency and 0.712 for a frequency-thresholded directly-follows measure on identical data; the compiler skill run blind reaches 0.992 on 250 of those edges. On AppWorld we replay released trajectories in the deterministic simulator to recover masked return values and measure the rule against 563 token edges at 0.993 precision - a self-consistency check, since replay injects tokens by a related heuristic. We compile two recurring intents: a Venmo money-request intent reduces 34 observed API calls to 11 runtime calls and, under leave-one-out execution against the benchmark's own state tests, passes 15 of 21, the failing fold escalating rather than acting because its required branch was never observed; and a Spotify/Todoist intent the compiler correctly refuses to compile, because an irreversible side effect is under-determined. We measure call reduction but not offline compilation cost, so we claim no net efficiency result.

</details>


### [129] [Studying Developer Perceptions on the Potential of CI Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02682)
*Osamah H. Alaini,Taher A. Ghaleb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过一项探索性调查研究，探讨GitHub开发者如何选择CI服务以及采用决策是由项目真实需求驱动还是受社会影响驱动，研究将联系约5000名活跃开发者（包括CI用户和非用户），并探讨CI推荐系统的开发者感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已识别出CI采用的障碍，但缺乏关于开发者如何选择CI服务、以及采用决策是由真实项目需求还是社会影响驱动的实证证据。

Method: 采用探索性调查研究方法，计划联系约5000名活跃的GitHub开发者（包括CI用户和非用户），围绕三个核心问题展开调查：CI采用和服务选择的驱动因素、CI必要性的认知与采用障碍、以及对自动化CI推荐系统的看法。

Result: 论文为探索性调查研究设计阶段，尚未呈现最终调查结果，但研究将为CI推荐系统开发研究人员和希望简化CI采用流程的开源项目从业者提供有价值的实证见解。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了CI服务选择和采用动机方面实证证据的空白，其发现将有助于开发更智能的CI推荐系统，并帮助开源项目更有效地采用CI服务。

Abstract: Continuous Integration (CI) is central to modern software development, yet developers often struggle to choose the most suitable CI service. Prior work has identified barriers to CI adoption but offers little empirical evidence on how developers select CI services or whether adoption decisions are driven by genuine project needs versus social influence. This paper presents an exploratory survey study addressing that gap. We aim to contact about 5,000 active GitHub developers, including both CI users and non-users. The study investigates: (1) what drives CI adoption and service selection, distinguishing need-driven from socially influenced motivations; (2) whether developers consider CI universally necessary or context-dependent and what barriers hinder adoption; and (3) developers' perceptions of automated CI recommendation systems. Our findings will inform researchers developing CI recommendation systems and practitioners aiming to streamline CI adoption in open-source projects.

</details>


### [130] [BulkPR-Bench: Benchmarking Queue-Level Governance of Interacting Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02685)
*Zetong Xiong,Qiao Zhao,Jun Zhang,Xueying Lyu,Zhi Li,Yixiang Tu,Xiaowen Yang,Yunjie Zhang,Yufeng Wang,Zhe Zhang,Kaize Yu,Hanwen Du,Zhongkai Sun,Zhuoxin Liu,Zekun Lin,Jianwen Yang,Ruining Chen,Ying Zhang,Tingxuan Pan,Ke Chen,Shubin Han,Chuanhao Sun,Yehua Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了BulkPR-Bench，一个可执行的基准测试，用于评估编码代理在批量PR队列中识别关系并返回最大安全有序子集的能力。基准包含581个候选PR和18个真实仓库快照，通过注册执行验证关系图。实验显示最优模型RDS为66.6%，仅8/324次运行能精确完成队列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码代理基准虽然涵盖长时程、端到端和交互式开发，但通常只关注单一目标结果或固定变更序列。实际开发中队列中的PR会相互影响，最大化安全交付需要联合决策合并哪些更改及合并顺序，现有基准无法评估这一能力。

Method: 构建BulkPR-Bench：在18个真实仓库冻结快照上创作581个候选PR；通过逐状态的仓库执行（含隐藏安全检查）验证黄金关系图；使用精确oracle计算最大安全子集；提出Relational Delivery Score（RDS）和Global Safety-Gated Yield（Global-SGY）两个指标；采用批量大小K=32的滚动发布协议进行评估。

Result: 六个模型中前三高的RDS为66.6%、62.0%和57.9%，强顺序基线为53.1%；仅8/324次模型运行能精确完成队列；临界关系召回率在35.2%到57.7%之间；即使提供黄金关系的诊断运行仍有显著提升空间。

Conclusion: 当前模型在关系组层面的提升尚未转化为可靠的整队列治理能力，模型在批量PR关系发现与安全排序方面仍有大量改进空间。

Abstract: Coding-agent benchmarks increasingly cover long-horizon, end-to-end, and interactive development, but typically retain one requested outcome or a fixed change sequence. Sequential policies can process a pull-request (PR) queue one candidate at a time, but when queued PRs interact, maximizing safe delivery can require jointly deciding which changes to merge and in what order. We introduce BulkPR-Bench, an executable benchmark in which an agent must recover consequential PR relations and return a large safe subset in executable order under a rolling-release protocol. The suite contains 581 newly authored candidate PRs on frozen snapshots of 18 real repositories. Registered state-by-state repository execution, including hidden safety checks, validates the gold relation graph; an exact oracle then computes the largest safe subset. Our primary metric, Relational Delivery Score (RDS), scores safe delivery and correct rejection over relation groups from the realized merge trace; Global Safety-Gated Yield (Global-SGY) separately measures strict delivery of the realized whole-queue plan. Under the buffered primary protocol with batch size $K=32$, the three highest RDS estimates among the six models are 66.6%, 62.0%, and 57.9%, compared with 53.1% for the strongest sequential baseline. Only 8 of 324 model runs complete a queue exactly. Critical-relation recall ranges from 35.2% to 57.7%, and diagnostic runs supplied with the gold relations show substantial remaining headroom. Gains on relation groups therefore do not yet translate into dependable whole-queue governance.

</details>


### [131] [PRWeaver: Evaluating LLM-Based Code Auditors against Long-Horizon Malicious Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02693)
*Yuekun Wang,Mingfei Cheng,Xiaofei Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PRWeaver基准，包含208个来自10个真实仓库的执行验证攻击，每种攻击有4种匹配的审查渲染（共832个）。研究发现提交边界拆分对逃避检测影响有限（最多5个百分点），而PR间交错（N=16）和载体融合可分别降低检测5-13和10-18个百分点。在N=24的全窗口审查下，检测率降至16-22%，远低于逐PR审查的50-60%。结果表明仅访问仓库历史不够，当良性变更与恶意变更共同占据审查上下文或陈述目的能合理解释攻击性差异时，隐蔽最为有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代码审计器越来越多地集成到PR工作流中，但其在面对跨仓库演化的对抗性变更时的可靠性仍缺乏理解。现有工作未系统评估攻击如何利用仓库历史分布和审查上下文来逃避检测。

Method: 构建PRWeaver基准，从10个真实仓库中提取208个执行验证的攻击，每种攻击实例化为4种匹配的审查渲染，共832个渲染。评估3个PR审计代理在6个审计器-模型系统上的检测性能，系统性地比较提交边界拆分、PR间交错、载体融合和审查窗口大小等因素的影响。

Result: 提交边界拆分对检测影响不超过5个百分点；PR间交错（N=16）降低检测5-13个百分点；载体融合降低10-18个百分点；全窗口审查（N=24）下检测率降至16-22%，而逐PR审查为50-60%。

Conclusion: 访问仓库历史本身不足以有效防御对抗性攻击。隐蔽攻击最有效的策略是：（1）使良性和恶意变更共同占据审计者的活跃审查上下文；（2）让所述目的能合理解释攻击性变更的存在。

Abstract: LLM-based code auditors are increasingly integrated into pull-request (PR) workflows, yet their reliability against adversarial changes distributed across repository evolution remains poorly understood. We introduce PRWeaver, a benchmark of 208 execution-validated attacks from ten real-world repositories, each instantiated under four matched review renderings (832 renderings in total). We evaluate three PR-auditing agents across six auditor-model systems. Across all systems, decomposing an attack changes detection by at most five percentage points, showing that commit boundaries alone do not explain evasion. In contrast, per-PR interleaving at $N=16$ and coherent carrier fusion reduce detection by 5-13 and 10-18 points, respectively. Under whole-window review at $N=24$, detection falls to 16-22%, compared with 50-60% under per-PR review. These results show that access to repository history is insufficient: concealment becomes most effective when benign and malicious changes jointly occupy the auditor's active review context or when the stated purpose plausibly accounts for the attack-bearing diff.

</details>


### [132] [Don't Regenerate, Debug: A Domain-Specific Agent for Repairing Near-Miss Hardware Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02712)
*Yansong Sun,Shenxiu Wu,Siyuan Chen,Runlin Hou,Junhao Qiu,Junming Cao,Shudi Shao,Zhichao Lu,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向硬件加速器（GPU/NPU）内核生成的领域专用调试代理，将"重新生成"范式转变为"调试"范式。通过检索模式、诊断插桩、防作弊检测和收敛控制等手段，该代理在调试成功率（Debug Pass@1 66.7%）上显著优于重新生成方法（Pass@1 25.9%），同时节省92.8%的token消耗，证明了从失败候选中提取可复用知识进行自主修复的巨大潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的内核生成pipeline会大量丢弃编译通过但数值验证失败的near-miss候选内核，这些候选包含宝贵的领域知识和推理/编译/执行的巨大投入。作者认为与其重新生成，不如调试——调试的搜索空间更小、反馈更密集，能更高效地修复这些近失算子。

Method: 提出一个领域专用调试代理，核心包括：(1) 通过检索模式与诊断插桩缓解知识稀缺；(2) 通过防作弊检测与全覆盖率评估确保完整性；(3) 通过收敛守卫与有界迭代控制成本。该代理对编译通过但数值验证失败的内核进行自主修复。

Result: Debug Pass@1达到66.7%，远高于Regenerate Avg Pass@1的25.9%和Regenerate Pass@3的40.7%；每次成功消耗的token比三次重新生成减少92.8%。组件消融实验表明知识库是恢复能力的关键驱动力，而完整性门控拒绝了工作流自身接受的12.5%-33.3%的成功案例。

Conclusion: 调试优于重新生成：调试不仅通过恢复反复重新生成都无法得到的算子来扩展能力边界，还能降低每个交付算子的成本。这代表了一种范式转变——将失败的候选内核视为可复用的知识资产而非丢弃的废物。

Abstract: Kernel generation for hardware accelerators such as GPUs and NPUs has become a proving ground for large language models (LLMs), and state-of-the-art systems raise correctness through pipelines that couple LLMs with agentic reinforcement learning and evolutionary search. Such pipelines generate, compile, and execute large numbers of candidate kernels, discarding most of them and forgoing the opportunity to distill failures into reusable knowledge. Many discarded candidates are near-miss operators that compile and run but fail numerical validation; each embodies genuine domain knowledge and a nontrivial investment in LLM inference, cross-compilation, and hardware execution. We argue for a paradigm shift: rather than regenerate, debug. Debugging is far more constrained than generating from scratch: the search space is small and feedback is dense. We present a domain-specific debug agent that addresses three core challenges in autonomous repair: mitigating knowledge scarcity through retrieved patterns and diagnostic instrumentation, ensuring integrity through anti-cheat detection and full-coverage evaluation, and controlling cost via convergence guards and bounded iteration. Debugging serves two complementary roles: it extends the capability frontier by recovering operators that repeated regeneration fails to produce, and it lowers cost per deliverable operator. Debug Pass@1 achieves 66.7% versus Regenerate Avg Pass@1's 25.9% and Regenerate Pass@3's 40.7%, while consuming 92.8% fewer tokens per success than three-trial regeneration. Component ablations show that the knowledge base drives recovery, while integrity gates reject 12.5-33.3% of the successes the workflow itself accepted.

</details>


### [133] [HyperFL: Query-Adaptive Representation Learning for Software Fault Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02967)
*Shuai Shao,Yiming Zeng,Yu Zhao,Tingting Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出HyperFL，一种查询自适应表示学习框架，通过轻量级超网络为查询编码器生成查询特定的LoRA参数，实现动态查询适应，在软件故障定位任务上比SOTA方法SweRank在MRR@10和Hit@1上分别提升13.3%和16.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于检索的软件故障定位方法对所有问题报告使用固定的查询表示，但真实世界的问题报告在长度、结构和调试信息上差异巨大，固定表示无法充分捕捉这种多样性。

Method: 提出HyperFL框架，采用轻量级超网络为查询编码器生成查询特定的LoRA参数，实现动态查询适应；代码编码器保持固定并可复用。超网络根据输入查询的独特特征动态调整LoRA参数，使不同特征的问题报告获得不同的编码表示。

Result: 在真实世界问题定位基准上的实验表明，HyperFL在多个嵌入骨干网络上稳定提升检索性能，相较于SOTA方法SweRank，函数级MRR@10相对提升13.3%，Hit@1相对提升16.7%。进一步分析显示HyperFL能为不同问题特征学习不同的适应模式。

Conclusion: 查询自适应表示学习对软件问题定位是有效的，HyperFL通过动态调整查询编码器参数来匹配不同类型问题报告的特征，优于静态查询表示方法。

Abstract: Software fault localization identifies the code locations responsible for reported issues and is a fundamental step toward automated debugging and program repair. Recent retrieval-based approaches formulate fault localization as a dense retrieval task by learning a shared embedding space between issue reports and source code. However, these methods encode all issue reports using a fixed query representation, despite the substantial diversity of real-world issue reports in length, structure, and debugging information. To address this limitation, we propose HyperFL, a query-adaptive representation learning framework for software fault localization. HyperFL employs a lightweight hypernetwork to generate query-specific LoRA parameters for the query encoder, enabling dynamic query adaptation while keeping the code encoder fixed and reusable. Experiments on a real-world issue localization benchmark demonstrate that HyperFL consistently improves retrieval performance across multiple embedding backbones, achieving up to 13.3% relative improvement in function-level MRR@10 and 16.7% relative improvement in Hit@1 over the state-of-the-art method SweRank. Further analysis shows that HyperFL learns distinct adaptation patterns for different issue characteristics, highlighting the effectiveness of query-adaptive representations for software issue localization.

</details>


### [134] [ConFL: Explainable Concurrent Fault Localization via Hierarchy-Guided LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02974)
*Shuai Shao,Dingbang Wang,Yiming Zeng,Tingting Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ConFL，一种可解释的并发故障定位框架，通过构建并发知识库（CKB）和LLM引导的分层检索来增强LLM推理能力，在八个大型Java项目上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅从bug报告定位并发bug具有挑战性，因为信息不完整、程序实体提及具有误导性、跨线程交互复杂，导致现有基于LLM的方法推理不稳定且可解释性有限。

Method: ConFL构建并发知识库（CKB），采用LLM引导的分层检索，从组件级逐步缩小到交互级并发上下文；使用交互级DSL显式编码跨线程交互，避免深层调用链遍历。

Result: 在八个大型Java项目的真实并发bug上，ConFL显著优于基于IR和LLM的基线方法，MRR达0.503，MAP达0.486，且对噪声bug报告、未见bug和不同LLM骨干具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将结构化并发知识融入LLM推理可以有效提升并发故障定位的准确性和可解释性，该框架在多种条件下均表现稳定。

Abstract: Localizing concurrent bugs from bug reports alone is challenging due to incomplete information, misleading program-entity mentions, and complex cross-thread interactions, causing existing LLM-based approaches to suffer from unstable reasoning and limited explainability. We propose ConFL, an explainable concurrent fault localization framework that augments LLM reasoning with structured concurrency knowledge. ConFL constructs a Concurrent Knowledge Base (CKB) from source code and performs LLM-guided hierarchical retrieval to progressively narrow the search space from components to interaction-level concurrency contexts. An interaction-level DSL explicitly encodes cross-thread interactions over shared resources, enabling focused reasoning without traversing deep call chains. Experiments on real-world concurrent bugs from eight large-scale Java projects show that ConFL significantly outperforms state-of-the-art IR-based and LLM-based baselines, achieving an MRR of 0.503 and a MAP of 0.486, while remaining robust to noisy bug reports, unseen bugs, and different LLM backbones.

</details>


### [135] [The Ground Is Shifting: A Reflection on the Foundations of Software Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03007)
*Thomas Bock,Audris Mockus,Bogdan Vasilescu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 软件测量研究长期以来依赖人工收集数据，后来转向利用版本控制等工具产生的追踪数据，但这隐含了"数据由专业开发者创建真实软件系统"的假设。随着AI代理开始使用这些工具，数据来源的假设被打破，因此需要系统性AI辅助复制程序重新审视关键发现，确保软件测量方法在新时代仍然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去六十年软件测量依赖劳动密集型人工数据收集，后来转向版本控制工具追踪数据虽然扩大了数据可用性，但隐含假设(数据由人类专业开发者生成)正被AI代理打破，这使得许多既有研究发现的有效性受到威胁，需要立即采取行动。

Method: 提出一个系统性的AI辅助复制程序，重新审视软件测量领域的关键发现，使用现代技术手段在新数据条件下验证既有方法论的可靠性，并开发适应新环境（包括AI生成数据）的测量方法。

Result: 论文提出的是一个研究方向和方法论框架而非具体实验结果，核心产出是识别出数据生成方式和分析方法的演变对软件测量研究发现的影响，以及提出AI辅助复制程序作为应对策略。

Conclusion: 为保持软件测量研究的现实意义，必须检测当代数据中基础假设被违反的情况，并开发在变化环境下仍然有效的新方法论。只有通过系统性的复制验证和方法的更新，软件测量才能继续为软件开发实践提供有价值的洞察。

Abstract: For most of the past six decades, software measurement relied on labor-intensive manual collection of proprietary data, which hampered progress. The shift to repurposing traces from version control and related tools dramatically expanded data availability$\unicode{x2014}$especially with the rise of open-source software$\unicode{x2014}$but hinged on an often unstated assumption: that these tools are used by professional developers to build genuine software systems. However, as trace-generating tools, data types and scale, and empirical methods have all evolved, it has become clear that changes in data generation and analytical approaches affect many prior findings about software development, maintenance, and evolution. With AI agents now actively using these same tools, the resulting traces frequently violate the original assumption of human origin. To preserve the relevance of software measurement research, immediate action is needed: We must detect when foundational assumptions are violated in contemporary data and develop new methodologies that remain valid under changed circumstances. To this end, we propose a systematic AI-assisted replication program that revisits key findings using modern techniques, aiming for methods that yield consistent results on current data to keep software measurement meaningful.

</details>


### [136] [LLM Serving in the Wild: An Empirical Study of Frameworks, Methods, and System Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03036)
*Forough Majidi,Mohammad Mehdi Morovati,Foutse Khomh,Heng Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对开源软件系统中的LLM服务框架（vLLM、SGLang、TensorRT-LLM、LMDeploy、FlashInfer）和服务方法进行了大规模实证研究，发现vLLM是最受欢迎的框架，并行计算、内存管理和网络剪枝是最常用的服务方法类别，多框架组合使用较少，且框架采用因模型类型、规模和部署场景而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已广泛集成到软件系统和AI服务中，高效的服务推理对软件工程至关重要，但已有研究提出了大量推理、优化和服务技术及框架，却缺乏对这些技术在实际中的采用情况的了解。

Method: 对开源软件系统进行大规模实证研究，识别并分析五个LLM特定服务框架（vLLM、SGLang、TensorRT-LLM、LMDeploy、FlashInfer），考察这些框架和技术的单独及组合采用情况、不同LLM类别间的采用差异，以及仓库在意图、焦点、用例和架构设计上的差异。

Result: vLLM在流行度和采用率上最为突出；并行计算、内存管理和网络剪枝是最常用的服务方法类别；多框架使用有限，开发者通常依赖单一服务框架，但组合框架能连接服务栈中的互补能力；框架采用在不同模型家族、模态、规模、领域专业化和部署环境中存在差异；仓库级分析显示LLM服务框架支持RL推理、多模态生成与理解、微服务和云基础设施等应用场景。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了对LLM服务框架在实践中采用情况的大规模经验特征描述，为研究人员、框架维护者和LLM系统从业人员提供了有价值的见解。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are integrated into software systems and AI services, making efficient LLM serving a concern for software engineering. Serving LLMs is challenging because inference requires computation, memory, GPU resources, and execution while maintaining latency and throughput. Although prior research has proposed LLM inference, optimization, and serving techniques and frameworks, little is known about how they are adopted in practice. In this study, we investigate the use of LLM serving frameworks and serving methods in open-source software systems. We identify and analyze five LLM-specific frameworks: vLLM, SGLang, TensorRT-LLM, LMDeploy, and FlashInfer. We examine how these frameworks and techniques are adopted individually and in combination, how adoption varies across categories of LLMs, and how repositories differ in intent, focus, use case, and architectural design. Our results show that vLLM is the most visible framework in popularity and adoption, while parallel computation, memory management, and network pruning are the most frequently used serving-method categories. Multi-framework usage is limited, suggesting that developers rely on a single serving framework; however, combined frameworks connect complementary capabilities across the serving stack. Framework adoption varies across model families, modalities, model sizes, domain specializations, and deployment settings. Repository-level analysis shows that LLM serving frameworks support applications and architectures, including Reinforcement Learning (RL)-based reasoning, multimodal generation and understanding, microservices, and cloud infrastructure. Overall, this study provides a large-scale empirical characterization of LLM serving framework adoption in practice and offers insights for researchers, framework maintainers, and practitioners working on LLM systems.

</details>


### [137] [Integration Barriers in Open-Source SSI Frameworks: An Exploratory Developer Experience Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03039)
*Breno Cerqueira Reis Nakamura,Arlindo Flavio da Conceição*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过探索性试点研究调查了开源自我主权身份（SSI）工具链的开发者体验（DX）。九名具有去中心化身份知识的开发者使用Walt.id、Traction和MetaMask执行核心凭证生命周期任务，研究发现被动操作（如凭证接收）相对成熟，但主动构建任务（尤其是schema定制）存在显著的架构摩擦，根源在于API抽象不足、环境配置脆弱和文档滞后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSI承诺实现数字身份的去中心化，但广泛采用受到集成复杂性和工具成熟度不足的阻碍。现有研究缺乏对SSI开源工具链开发者体验的系统性调查，因此需要探索早期集成者在实际任务中遇到的障碍，以推动生态系统的发展。
