Method: 采用探索性试点研究方法，招募九名具有去中心化身份概念知识的开发者（代表早期集成者），使用Walt.id、Traction和MetaMask三种开源SSI工具执行核心凭证生命周期任务。通过开放式的定性主题分析（qualitative thematic analysis）结合任务级难度评分来揭示反复出现的集成障碍。

Result: 研究发现存在关键的抽象鸿沟：被动操作（如凭证接收）相对成熟，但主动构建任务（尤其是schema定制）暴露出显著的架构摩擦。这些障碍源于API抽象不足、脆弱的环境配置以及无法跟上生态系统快速演进的文档，反映了当前框架的结构性设计决策问题。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了当前SSI开源生态系统中存在的结构性集成障碍，并针对已识别的缺口提出三个开发者工具的架构转变方向：基于Web的沙箱环境、AI辅助的schema生成器以及可执行的文档策略。

Abstract: Self-Sovereign Identity (SSI) promises to decentralize digital identity, but widespread adoption remains hindered by integration complexity and tooling immaturity. This paper investigates the Developer Experience (DX) of open-source SSI tooling through an exploratory probe study. Nine developers with prior knowledge of decentralized identity concepts, representing early integrators building SSI applications, attempted core credential lifecycle tasks using Walt.id, Traction, and MetaMask. Our goal was to surface recurring integration barriers through qualitative thematic analysis of open-ended developer reports, complemented by task-level difficulty ratings. Our findings reveal a critical abstraction gap: while passive operations like credential receipt are relatively mature, active construction tasks, particularly schema customization, expose significant architectural friction. We identify that these barriers stem from inadequate API abstractions, brittle environment configurations, and documentation that fails to track the ecosystem's rapid evolution. These issues reflect structural design decisions in current frameworks. This study characterizes the structural integration barriers in the current SSI open-source ecosystem. To address the identified gaps, we propose three architectural shifts for developer tooling: web-based sandboxes, AI-assisted schema generators, and executable documentation strategies.

</details>


### [138] [Efficient Grammar-Constrained Decoding via Parser Stack Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03065)
*Yongmin Li,Yihong Dong,Jia Li,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出PSC方法，一种新型的语法约束解码方法，通过将词汇表中所有token的接受条件合并为解析器栈上的单一分类器，使每次解码步骤只需检查解析器栈一次即可计算完整的词汇掩码，时间复杂度与词汇表大小无关，掩码计算速度比基线快最多700倍，端到端吞吐量接近无约束解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语法约束解码（GCD）方法在在线计算开销上存在问题，延迟通常随词汇表大小线性增长，这限制了LLM的吞吐量，尤其是对于词汇量大的模型。

Method: PSC方法在预处理阶段将所有词汇token的接受条件合并为解析器栈上的单一分类器，在解码时只需检查解析器栈一次即可获得完整的词汇掩码，时间复杂度与词汇表大小无关。同时提供了预处理开销分析和盈亏平衡点分析，帮助用户决定是否自行预处理。

Result: 在复杂编程语言语法上，PSC的掩码计算速度比基线快高达700倍；在符合schema的JSON上快达30倍；端到端LLM吞吐量接近无约束解码。

Conclusion: PSC显著提升了语法约束解码的效率，使受约束生成的实际吞吐量接近无约束解码，为大型词汇量模型的文法约束生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: LLMs are widely used to generate structured output like source code or JSON. Grammar-constrained decoding (GCD) can guarantee the syntactic validity of the generated output, by masking out tokens that violate rules specified by a context-free grammar. However, the online computational overhead of existing GCD methods, with latency typically scaling linearly with vocabulary size, limits the throughput of LLMs, especially for models with large vocabularies. To address this issue, we propose PSC, a novel grammar-constrained decoding method. By combining acceptance conditions of all vocabulary tokens into a single classifier of the parser stack during preprocessing, PSC can compute the complete vocabulary mask by checking the parser stack exactly once per decoding step, with time complexity independent of the vocabulary size. Experiments show that PSC computes masks up to 700$\times$ faster than baselines on complex programming language grammars, and up to 30$\times$ faster for schema-conformant JSON; end-to-end LLM throughput with PSC approaches that of unconstrained decoding. We analyze the preprocessing overhead for preprocessing providers and decoding users, and provide a break-even point analysis to help users decide whether to do preprocessing by themselves.

</details>


### [139] [Cross-Ecosystem Bug Classification in Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03173)
*Mir Mohammad Yousuf,Shabir Ahmad Sofi,Bisma Majid*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Qiskit（12,910个issue）及其他11个量子软件仓库（4,613个issue）进行了跨生态系统的缺陷比较研究，使用基于规则的分类框架分析缺陷类型、类别、严重程度和质量属性。研究发现经典缺陷占主导（67%），量子特定缺陷占27-30%，且规则框架在缺陷分类上优于四种监督机器学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件工程因经典与量子组件的交互而产生复杂且难以理解的缺陷模式，但缺乏跨生态系统的缺陷系统化对比研究，这阻碍了量子生态系统中测试、调试和质量保证的进展。

Method: 采用基于规则的分类框架，对12,910个Qiskit issue和11个其他仓库（包括Cirq和PyQuil）的4,613个issue进行分析，按缺陷类型、类别、严重程度、质量属性和量子特定子类型进行分类，并与四种监督机器学习基线进行对比验证。

Result: 经典缺陷占67%为主导，量子特定缺陷占27-30%。Qiskit仓库表现出更多兼容性相关缺陷，其他生态系统语法和量子特定缺陷率更高。门和电路问题主导量子特定缺陷。规则框架优于机器学习基线，尤其在细粒度量子特定子类型上。纵向分析（2017-2025）显示量子特定缺陷保持相对稳定而非持续增长。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了量子软件缺陷分布的首次跨生态系统比较，验证了可解释、可自动化的规则框架的实用性，可为量子软件的测试、调试和质量保证提供指导。

Abstract: Quantum software engineering faces unique challenges due to the interaction of classical and quantum components, which produce complex and often poorly understood bug patterns. Characterizing these bugs is essential for advancing testing, debugging, and quality assurance in quantum ecosystems. This paper presents a comparative study of 12,910 issues from Qiskit and 4,613 issues from 11 additional repositories, including Cirq and PyQuil. Using a rule-based classification framework, we analyze bugs by type, category, severity, quality attributes, and quantum-specific subtypes. Results show that classical bugs consistently dominate (67%) across ecosystems, while quantum-specific bugs account for 27-30%. Ecosystem-specific trends emerge: Qiskit repositories exhibit more compatibility related bugs, whereas other ecosystems show higher syntax and quantum-specific bug rates. Across both ecosystems, gate and circuit issues dominate quantum-specific bugs, though non-Qiskit projects reveal broader diversity, including algorithmic, resource, and hybrid-interface issues. Statistical validation confirms that the framework generalizes at the bug-type level while detecting significant variations at finer levels. Benchmarking against four supervised machine-learning baselines further shows that the rule-based framework consistently outperforms data-driven models, particularly for fine-grained quantum-specific subtypes, while longitudinal analysis (2017-2025) indicates that quantum- specific bugs remain relatively stable over time rather than exhibiting a steady increase. This study provides the first cross- ecosystem comparison of bug distributions in quantum software, demonstrating the utility of an interpretable, automation-ready, rule-based framework for guiding testing, debugging, and quality assurance.

</details>


### [140] [Fail-Fast, Restart-Smart: Early Failure Prediction and Restart for SWE Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03222)
*Chenyu Wang,Yunbo Lyu,Junda He,Zhou Yang,Chenxing Zhong,Yaniv Harel,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出FailFast-RestartSmart两阶段控制器，通过轻量级0.6B监控器预测SWE代理轨迹失败，并在报警时以相同策略重启并保留仓库diff作为可选项，在SWE-bench Verified上显著节省执行令牌并提升分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程代理在解决仓库级问题时轨迹越来越长且成本高昂；失败的运行往往更长且存在冗余探索或循环，暗示部分失败在完成前可被检测到。但提前终止可能打断本会成功的轨迹，而失败的轨迹也可能包含有用的编辑。

Method: 提出两阶段控制器：FailFast使用0.6B轻量级监控器，仅基于可观察的轨迹前缀（无需策略logits或隐藏状态）预测失败；当报警时，RestartSmart启动一个新的相同策略轨迹（不保留提示历史），并将被中断的仓库diff作为可选覆盖层供代理检查、应用或丢弃。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，仅用Qwen3.6-27B轨迹训练的监控器可迁移到三种其他策略（包括闭源API模型），在5%假阳性率下节省14.6%-20.4%执行令牌；在Qwen3.6-27B上20.4%的节省超过AgentStop的12.5%。在25%假阳性率下，RestartSmart将Qwen3.6-27B的分辨率从66.6%提升到71.8%，而冷重启仅达66.8%。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持"提前终止+顺序相同策略恢复"这一范式，即早期失败检测配合同策略重启（并可选利用已中断轨迹的diff）能有效提升代理效率和成功率。

Abstract: Software engineering (SWE) agents resolve repository-level issues through long trajectories that grow increasingly expensive as context accumulates. Failed runs tend to be longer and exhibit redundant exploration or looping, suggesting that some failures may be detectable before completion. Early termination, however, risks interrupting trajectories that would otherwise succeed; conversely, an unsuccessful trajectory may still contain useful repository edits. We present FailFast-RestartSmart, a two-stage controller for a single active trajectory. FailFast is a lightweight 0.6B monitor trained with terminal and dense fail-to-pass supervision to predict failure from observable prefixes without policy logits or hidden states. Upon an alarm, RestartSmart launches a fresh same-policy rollout without prior prompt history and offers the interrupted repository diff as an optional overlay that the agent may inspect, apply, or discard. On SWE-bench Verified, a monitor trained solely on Qwen3.6-27B trajectories transfers to three other policies, including a closed-API model, and saves 14.6%-20.4% of execution tokens at a target 5% false-positive rate; on Qwen3.6-27B, its 20.4% saving exceeds the 12.5% achieved by our per-step AgentStop adaptation. At a target 25% false-positive rate, RestartSmart raises Qwen3.6-27B resolution from 66.6% to 71.8%, whereas cold restart reaches only 66.8%. Together, these results support early termination with sequential same-policy recovery.

</details>


### [141] [NotDec: WebAssembly Decompilation With Inter-Procedural Type Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03286)
*Jikai Wang,Ningyu He,Tianming Liu,Junhai Wang,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: NotDec是一个先进的WebAssembly反编译框架，通过扩展类型检查算法、使用Retypd跨过程类型恢复和Memory SSA技术，实现高可读性、语义一致的C代码生成，在重编译成功率和类型恢复准确性上大幅超越现有工具（如Ghidra）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebAssembly在浏览器、容器、物联网和Serverless平台中广泛使用，已成为通用底层字节码标准，但其隐藏漏洞和恶意行为的审计至关重要。现有反编译工具存在输出冗长、可读性差和类型恢复能力有限的问题。

Method: NotDec框架包含三个核心部分：1) 扩展WebAssembly类型检查算法将字节码提升为基于SSA的中间表示（IR）；2) 应用跨过程类型恢复算法Retypd，结合指针和数值类型区分方法恢复复杂数据结构；3) 利用Memory SSA和保持语义的结构化控制流分析，生成可读且语义一致的C代码。

Result: NotDec在5,241个Juliet样本和所有Howard数据集程序上实现100%的重编译成功率，远超Ghidra的45.95%。在类型恢复准确性上，NotDec对真实世界程序的结构体成员访问恢复率达85.33%，远超Ghidra的9.24%。其过程内变体NotDec_F在大型二进制文件上内存消耗不到Ghidra的一半，未优化二进制文件上执行时间减少高达97%。

Conclusion: NotDec在反编译质量和类型恢复能力上显著优于现有工具，展示了在处理WebAssembly安全审计方面的巨大潜力；尽管完整的跨过程版本在大二进制文件上存在可扩展性挑战，但过程内变体NotDec_F在效率和资源消耗上表现出色，为不同场景提供了灵活选择。

Abstract: With WebAssembly widely supported in browsers, containers, IoT devices, and serverless platforms and increasingly adopted as a universal low-level bytecode standard, auditing its hidden vulnerabilities and malicious intentions has become critical. Decompiling existing WebAssembly modules can help security researchers and end users understand binary behavior, but current tools suffer from verbose result, poor readability, and limited type recovery.
  We present NotDec, an advanced WebAssembly decompilation framework. NotDec extends the WebAssembly type checking algorithm to lift bytecode into an SSA-based IR, applies the inter-procedural type recovery algorithm Retypd with pointer and numeric value differentiation methods to recover complex data structures, and leverages Memory SSA alongside semantics-preserving structured control-flow analysis to emit readable, semantically consistent C code.
  NotDec achieves 100% recompilation success rate on all 5,241 Juliet samples and all Howard dataset programs, significantly outperforming baselines including Ghidra (45.95% success rate). On type recovery accuracy, NotDec recovers 85.33% of struct member accesses in real-world programs, vastly exceeding Ghidra's 9.24%. While the full inter-procedural version faces scalability challenges on large binaries, the intra-procedural variant NotDec_F demonstrates superior efficiency, consuming less than half of Ghidra's memory and up to 97% less execution time on unoptimized binaries.

</details>


### [142] [Evaluating LLM Trade-offs for Enterprise Automation: Lessons from Workflow Generation in a Production Enterprise Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03311)
*Xavier Wrenn,Radoslav Raykov,Aleksandar Angelov,Hirokuni Kitahara,Yuji Watanabe,Anca Sailer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了六种大语言模型在生产企业平台中生成AI驱动工作流的性能，通过29个真实IT自动化场景、2,784次运行测试了两种生成流水线架构。分段式生成流水线将结构成功率从31.5-82.8%提升至74.1-97.8%，使小型模型（如mistral-small）达到生产可用性，实现了模型无关的可扩展自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业合规管理需要快速适应不断演变的监管框架和严格的修复SLA，而传统静态编排器在混合云环境中无法满足事件驱动评估对自动化代码在秒级内适应运行时上下文的动态需求。

Method: 在生产企业平台中评估六种LLM的AI驱动工作流生成能力，基准测试涵盖29个真实IT自动化场景、两种生成流水线架构（单体式与分段式）、每种prompt-model-pipeline配置运行8次，共2,784次运行。分段式流水线将工作流构建分解为变量脚手架、基础块组装和嵌套块生成三个步骤。

Result: 初始单体流水线结构成功率为31.5-82.8%，分段式流水线将成功率提升至74.1-97.8%。mistral-medium-2505和gpt-oss-120b达到最高结构成功率（96.1%和97.8%），但mistral-small以95.7%的成功率和USD 0.01/工作流的低成本实现生产可行性。成本范围为USD 0.008-0.20/工作流，延迟低于50秒。

Conclusion: 分段式分解使较小模型达到生产可用性，消除对昂贵前沿模型的依赖。部署经验表明需将结构有效性与语义正确性分离考量，为云工程提供模型无关、可扩展的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Enterprise compliance management requires rapid adaptation to evolving regulatory frameworks (e.g., DORA, AI RMF, FedRAMP) and tight remediation SLAs. Traditional static orchestrators often fail in hybrid cloud environments where event-driven assessments demand that automation code adapt to runtime context in seconds. This paper presents lessons learned from evaluating six large language models for AI-driven workflow generation in a production enterprise platform, benchmarked across 29 real-world IT automation scenarios, two generation pipeline architectures, and eight independent runs per prompt-model-pipeline configuration (2,784 runs total).
  Our initial pipeline used monolithic workflow generation, achieving 31.5-82.8% structural success rates (JSON schema validity and correct UI rendering), with most models struggling on complex JSON generation. We developed a redesigned piecewise pipeline that decomposes workflow construction into variable scaffolding, base block assembly, and nested block generation, raising structural success to 74.1-97.8% across all models.
  We analyze production tradeoffs including cost (USD 0.008-0.20 per workflow), latency (under 50s for interactive use), and model selection. Piecewise decomposition enables smaller models (e.g., mistral-small at 95.7% structural success and USD 0.01 per workflow) to reach production viability, removing dependency on expensive frontier models. While mistral-medium-2505 and gpt-oss-120b achieved the highest structural success (96.1% and 97.8%), mistral-medium-2505 carries a 19x cost premium versus mistral-small. Our deployment lessons highlight the need to separate structural validity from semantic correctness (logical fulfillment of user intent) and provide a solution for model-agnostic, scalable automation in cloud engineering.

</details>


### [143] [Making AI Visible, Not Vanished: How AI Policies Reshape Developer Experience on GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03329)
*Yunqi Chen,Thomas Zimmermann,Bianca Trinkenreich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对29,624个GitHub仓库进行大规模实证研究，识别出385个采用AI治理政策的开源项目，提出TRACE框架（透明度、责任、归属、约束、执行）对AI治理政策进行五维分类，并发现AI治理主要规范而非禁止AI辅助开发，强调透明度与责任的政策比限制性政策能带来更好的社区和代码质量效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正在快速改变开源软件的开发方式，许多项目开始引入AI辅助贡献的政策，但关于这些政策之间的差异及其对开发者体验的影响知之甚少。这是首个关于开源软件中AI治理政策的大规模实证研究。

Method: 分析了29,624个GitHub仓库，识别出385个采用AI政策的项目。提出TRACE框架（涵盖透明度、责任、归属、约束、执行五个治理维度），将政策分为五类治理家族，并使用倾向性评分匹配和纵向双重差分分析评估政策效果。

Result: 研究发现AI治理主要规范而非禁止AI辅助开发。政策采纳带来了维护者参与度提升、AI披露增加、更丰富的评审互动以及更好的代码质量，同时AI辅助贡献仍在持续增长。强调透明度和责任的政策比单纯的限制性方法产生了更强的社区和质量效果。

Conclusion: 不同AI治理策略会显著影响开发者体验，研究为开源社区设计有效的AI政策提供了实证依据，表明治理设计至关重要——强调透明度与责任的政策效果优于限制性政策。

Abstract: Generative AI is rapidly reshaping Open Source Software (OSS) software development,prompting projects to introduce policies governing AI-assisted contributions. However, little is known about how these policies differ or whether they influence developer experience. We present the first large-scale empirical study of AI governance policies in OSS. Analyzing 29,624 GitHub repositories, we identify 385 projects that adopted AI policies and derive TRACE, a framework capturing five governance dimensions: Transparency, Responsibility, Attribution, Constraints, and Enforcement. We further classify policies into five governance families and estimate their effects using propensity-score matching and longitudinal difference-in-differences analysis. Our results show that AI governance primarily regulates rather than prohibits AI-assisted development. Policy adoption brings maintainer engagement, increased AI disclosure, richer review interactions, and improved code quality while AI-assisted contributions continue to grow. Governance design matters: policies emphasizing transparency and responsibility produced stronger community and quality outcomes than restrictive approaches alone. Our findings show how different AI governance strategies shape developer experience and provide evidence to help OSS communities design effective AI policies.

</details>


### [144] [Assessing Behavioral Validation in UI Component Test Suites Using Inferred Metamorphic Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03337)
*Yu Pei,Cunming Zhang,Jeongju Sohn,Mike Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于蜕变关系（MR）的框架，将推断出的MR作为经验性行为参考而非完整规范，用于评估UI组件测试套件。框架通过UI特定分类法推断组件特定MR，结合确定性分析与语义分析将测试与MR对齐，计算关系级别的MR覆盖率指标。评估表明MR覆盖率（42.5%-47.6%）显著低于MR触及率，揭示现有测试套件存在大量弱预言（weak-oracle）情况，MR覆盖率能补充传统执行覆盖率未反映的行为缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UI组件库的测试评估通常依赖语句覆盖率和分支覆盖率等执行指标，但这些指标无法反映测试是否验证了组件API和文档所隐含的行为关系，缺乏对行为验证层面的评估手段。

Method: 提出MR-based框架：利用UI特定分类法从组件源码、文档和测试中推断组件特定蜕变关系；通过混合确定性分析与语义分析将测试与推断的MR对齐；计算关系级别的MR覆盖率指标（如MR Cover和MR Touch）；并通过人工验证推断的MR空间与测试-MR对齐的准确性。

Result: 现有测试套件在实际执行中触及的行为关系远多于其明确验证的关系：三种LLM配置下MR覆盖率在42.5%-47.6%之间且始终低于MR触及率。未覆盖的关系主要是弱预言案例——行为被触发但缺少明确验证。MR覆盖率能揭示语句和分支覆盖率未反映的行为缺口。大多数issue描述可映射到推断的MR关系类型，MR标签在MR相关故障检测中呈现趋势性关联。

Conclusion: MR覆盖率提供了传统执行覆盖率之外的互补性关系级视角，可用于评估现代UI组件测试中的行为验证充分性，对测试质量评估具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: UI component libraries are commonly assessed using execution-based metrics such as statement and branch coverage, yet these metrics provide limited insight into whether tests verify the behavioral relations implied by component APIs and documentation. This paper presents an MR-based framework that uses inferred metamorphic relations (MRs) as an empirical behavioral reference, rather than a complete specification, for assessing UI component test suites. Given a component's source, documentation, and tests, the framework infers component-specific MRs using a UI-specific taxonomy, aligns tests with the inferred relations through hybrid deterministic and semantic analysis, and computes relation-level MR coverage metrics. We manually validate both the inferred MR space and the test--MR alignment. Our evaluation shows that existing test suites exercise substantially more behavioral relations than they explicitly validate: MR Cover remains between 42.5% and 47.6% across three LLM configurations and consistently below MR Touch. Most uncovered relations are weak-oracle cases, where behaviors are exercised but lack explicit behavioral validation. MR coverage also complements execution-based coverage by revealing behavioral gaps not reflected by statement or branch coverage alone. We further assess practical relevance through issue-description mapping, oracle strengthening, and MR-relevant injected faults. Most reported issue descriptions can be mapped to inferred MR relation types; weak-oracle relations often expose missing validation evidence; and MR labels show a trend in MR-relevant fault detection. Overall, MR coverage provides a complementary relation-level perspective for assessing behavioral validation in modern UI component testing.

</details>


### [145] [Route-Align-Verify for Functional Correctness in Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03341)
*Erxue Zhou,Jingxiang Meng,Aofan Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RAV框架，通过"路由-对齐-验证"三阶段协同策略，在固定骨干模型（不改架构）的情况下显著提升代码生成的功能正确性，在MBPP基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在代码生成上虽有显著进步，但在异构编程任务中，单一提示策略和单次直接生成的输出往往不足以实现强功能正确性，需要更精细化的协同策略。

Method: RAV框架包含三个阶段：(1) Route：在生成前进行任务感知的提示路由；(2) Align：通过对齐的LoRA适配，减少微调提示与推理提示之间的不匹配；(3) Verify：通过执行多个候选输出并对照可见公共测试来选择最终结果。

Result: 在MBPP Sanitized上达到0.8911，在MBPP Full上达到0.8520，较基础模型分别提升6.35和9.92个百分点。消融实验表明，任务感知路由和对齐适配与基于执行的验证结合时效果显著增强。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化任务提示方式、模型适配方式和最终输出选择方式，无需修改骨干架构即可有意义地提升代码生成的功能正确性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have substantially improved code generation, yet achieving strong functional correctness remains difficult, especially for heterogeneous programming tasks where a single prompting strategy and a single directly generated output are often insufficient. In this paper, we present RAV, a lightweight and modular framework that improves code generation with a fixed backbone model through three coordinated stages: Route, which applies task-aware prompt routing before generation; Align, which reduces the mismatch between fine-tuning prompts and inference-time prompts through aligned LoRA adaptation; and Verify, which selects the final output by executing multiple candidates against visible public tests.
  We evaluate RAV on the MBPP benchmark under both the sanitized and full settings. The complete RAV pipeline achieves the best performance among all evaluated configurations, reaching 0.8911 on MBPP Sanitized and 0.8520 on MBPP Full. Compared with the base model, these results represent improvements of 6.35 and 9.92 percentage points, respectively. Component-wise ablation experiments further show that task-aware routing and aligned adaptation become substantially more effective when combined with execution-based verification. Additional robustness and contamination analyses support the reliability of the observed improvements. Overall, the results indicate that functional correctness in code generation can be meaningfully improved without modifying the backbone architecture, by jointly optimizing how tasks are prompted, how the model is adapted, and how final outputs are selected.

</details>


### [146] [Self-Evolving Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03392)
*Hao Zhou,Haichuan Hu,Ye Shang,Quanjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统调研了自进化编码代理（self-evolving coding agents）这一新兴领域，定义了其概念边界，提出了以对象为中心的分类体系，并从进化时机和软件特定证据两个正交视角进行补充分析，最后总结了该领域面临的主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码代理在部署后基本保持静态，但软件开发是动态的、富含反馈的过程，仓库会演变、依赖会变化、测试会失败，修复尝试会留下可复用的经验。这种张力促使对自进化编码代理的研究日益增多，但该领域缺乏系统性梳理。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法：首先定义自进化编码代理并将其与常规编码代理和通用自进化代理区分；然后开发以对象为中心的分类体系，描述系统中进化的内容；再补充两个正交视角（进化时机和驱动进化的软件特定证据）；最后围绕这些维度组织现有工作。

Result: 发现可执行反馈、仓库级上下文和编码轨迹使软件工程成为代理自进化的天然领域，但也带来了反馈可靠性、基准过拟合、安全性、可维护性、成本和泛化等新挑战。

Conclusion: 该综述旨在澄清自进化编码代理的概念边界，为设计更自适应、更可靠、更具软件感知能力的代理系统提供基础，并提供了开源文献资源库。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly embedded in software engineering workflows as coding agents that can inspect repositories, invoke tools, execute tests, debug failures, and generate patches. Yet most existing agents remain largely static after deployment, even though software development is a dynamic, feedback-rich process in which repositories evolve, dependencies change, tests fail, and repair attempts leave reusable experience. This tension has motivated a growing body of work on self-evolving coding agents, where the agent improves its future behavior by updating its framework, memory, skills, tools, models, or collaboration structures from prior coding interactions. In this survey, we provide a systematic synthesis of this emerging area. We first define self-evolving coding agents and distinguish them from conventional coding agents and general self-evolving agents. We then develop an object-centered taxonomy that characterizes what evolves in these systems, and complement it with two orthogonal perspectives: when evolution occurs and what software-specific evidence drives it. Across the literature, we find that executable feedback, repository-level context, and coding trajectories give software engineering a distinctive role as a natural domain for agent self-evolution, but also introduce new challenges in feedback reliability, benchmark overfitting, safety, maintainability, cost, and generalization. By organizing existing work around these dimensions, this survey aims to clarify the conceptual boundaries of self-evolving coding agents and provide a foundation for designing more adaptive, reliable, and software-aware agentic systems. The papers we collect can be found at https://github.com/zhouhao1024/Awesome-Self-Evolving-Coding-Agents.

</details>


### [147] [When AI Joins the Team! A Model of How AI Adoption Relates To Social Patterns in Software Engineering Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03462)
*Giusy Annunziata,Rudrajit Choudhuri,Anita Sarma,Gemma Catolino,Filomena Ferrucci*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究基于Transactive Memory Systems (TMS)理论，通过对152名使用AI工具的软件专业人员的调查数据，验证了AI采用与社区气味（community smells）之间的关系并非单一方向，而是根据工作类型（专业化或协调）通过不同机制发挥作用：在专业化工作中，AI通过增强知识共享的同伴互动减少社区气味；在协调工作中，AI直接提升沟通质量，补充而非替代人类互动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助开发工具的日益普及正在改变软件团队的协作、知识共享和协调方式，然而其对社会动态（尤其是社区气味这类反映协调与沟通障碍的社会技术反模式）的影响尚未得到充分探索，学术界对AI采用是增加还是减少社区气味及其作用机制缺乏清晰认识。

Method: 本研究以TMS理论为基础，从专业化和协调两个维度验证了人-AI与人-人交互的测量工具，并在152名使用AI工具的软件专业人员调查数据上测试了五个PLS-SEM模型；社区气味构念来源于文献并经过专家调查和因子分析验证。

Result: AI采用与社区气味之间的关系不是单一的，而是取决于工作类型：在专业化工作中，AI与更高的知识共享同伴互动相关，进而与更少的社区气味相关；在协调工作中，AI与更高的沟通质量直接相关，补充而非替代人类互动。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一项经过实证验证的、基于TMS的模型，表明AI与社区气味的关系取决于协作类型，同时提供了可复用的测量工具以及对研究和实践的循证启示。

Abstract: Context: The growing adoption of AI-assisted development tools is changing how software teams collaborate, share knowledge, and coordinate, yet its consequences for team social dynamics remain largely unexplored. Gap: It is unclear whether AI adoption is associated with an increase or reduction in community smells,socio-technical anti-patterns reflecting coordination and communication breakdowns,and through which mechanisms. Method: Grounded in Transactive Memory Systems (TMS) theory, we validate instruments for HumanAI and HumanHuman interaction along two TMS dimensions, Specialization and Coordination, and test five PLS-SEM models on survey data from 152 software professionals using AI tools. Community smell constructs were derived from the literature and validated through expert surveys and factor analysis. Results: AI adoption relates to community smells not in a single way, but through mechanisms depending on the work. In specialization work, AI is associated with higher knowledge-sharing peer interaction, which is in turn associated with fewer smells. In coordination work, AI is directly associated with higher communication quality, complementing rather than replacing human interaction. Contributions: We provide an empirically validated, TMS-grounded model showing that the AIcommunity-smell relationship is contingent on the type of collaboration, with a reusable instrument and evidence-based implications for research and practice.

</details>


### [148] [Leveraging System-Level Observations to Inform Bayesian Learning of Model Parameters for Quantitative Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03489)
*Simos Gerasimou,Xingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EPIK是一种将贝叶斯学习与定量验证相结合的方法，通过从可观测的系统级属性中提取并嵌入先验知识，解决贝叶斯推断中先验知识不准确或信息不足导致验证结果错误的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在贝叶斯学习与定量验证结合的分析框架中，验证结果的准确性和鲁棒性强烈依赖于贝叶斯推断所使用的先验知识。然而，不准确或信息不足的先验知识会负面影响定量分析，导致错误的验证结果。现有方法通常需要特定于形式模型转移参数的先验知识，缺乏直接可观测且具有真实世界语义的先验来源。

Method: EPIK方法通过两个关键步骤实现先验知识的提取与嵌入：首先，利用系统级可直接观测的属性（这些属性与真实世界的语义相关联），构建一个两重优化问题来推导未知转移参数的分布；然后，将这些推导出的分布嵌入到定量验证框架中，用于验证新的或难以测量的属性。

Result: 通过使用多种真实世界案例研究的变体和多种EPIK实例化进行的详细实验评估，证明了该方法在有效性、灵活性和通用性方面的优势。

Conclusion: EPIK提供了一种新颖且实用的解决方案，利用系统级可观测属性来提取和嵌入先验知识，克服了传统方法对形式模型参数先验知识的依赖，从而提高了定量验证的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Combining Bayesian learning and quantitative verification is a powerful toolset for analysing key quantitative properties of software systems, like reliability and response time. However, the accuracy and robustness of verification results strongly depend on the prior knowledge (PK) underlying Bayesian inference. This knowledge reflects original beliefs about the probability of events and typically depends on domain expertise. Using inaccurate or uninformative PK can negatively affect quantitative analysis, yielding incorrect verification results. Our EPIK approach tackles this important challenge by eliciting and embedding PK in quantitative verification equipped with Bayesian estimators. Unlike existing approaches that require PK on formal model transition parameters, EPIK leverages system-level properties that are directly observable and are linked to real-world semantics. EPIK formulates a twofold optimisation problem to derive the distributions of unknown transition parameters and then embeds these distributions to verify new or difficult-to-measure (elusive) properties. The detailed experimental evaluation using multiple variants of real-world case studies and diverse EPIK instantiations shows its effectiveness, flexibility and generality.

</details>


### [149] [CodeAssay: A Multi-Metric Benchmark with Audited Ground Truth for LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03535)
*Shahbaz Siddeeq,Muhammad Waseem,Umar Subhan Malhi,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CodeAssay，一个基于分类法的185个Python任务基准测试，用于评估LLM代码生成。该基准结合了审计后的真实答案、公开/隐藏测试集、变异测试验证和代码属性度量，发现审计后修正了9%的正确性标签，并将模型间差距从11.9扩大到23.7个百分点，凸显了可靠评估需要验证的真实答案和受保护的测试集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成评估依赖于基于测试的基准，但这些评估的可靠性取决于其参考实现和测试集的质量。同时，基于测试的正确性只能捕获生成代码的部分可观察属性，缺乏对代码质量等多维度的考量。

Method: 构建了包含185个Python任务的CodeAssay基准，覆盖十个软件工程类别。采用审计后的ground truth、生成和修复的公开测试、用于评分的隐藏测试、基于变异的测试套件验证以及选定的代码属性度量。

Result: 审计后重新评分改变了1890个正确性标签中的170个（9.0%），模型间最好到最差差距从11.9个百分点增加到23.7个百分点，但总体正确率几乎不变。完整和隐藏测试套件的变异得分分别为82.6%和74.8%。七个专有LLM的标准提示正确率从77.3%到98.9%不等，21对模型中有12对存在显著差异。安全导向的提示未产生显著的正确性变化，但一致增加了程序长度和圈复杂度。

Conclusion: 可靠的LLM生成代码评估需要经过验证的真实答案、受保护的测试集和多个明确解释的度量指标。CodeAssay为AI增强软件开发中的循证模型评估提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly evaluated for code generation using test-based benchmarks. The validity of such evaluations depends on the reliability of their references and tests, while test-based correctness captures only part of the observable properties of generated code. We present CodeAssay, a taxonomy-first benchmark of 185 Python tasks across ten software-engineering categories. It combines audited ground truth, public tests for generation and repair, hidden tests for grading, mutation-based test-suite validation, and selected code-property measures. Regrading fixed model outputs after the audit changed 170 of 1,890 correctness labels (9.0%) and increased the measured best-to-worst model spread from 11.9 to 23.7 percentage points, although aggregate correctness remained nearly unchanged. The complete and hidden test suites achieved mutation scores of 82.6% and 74.8%, respectively. Across seven proprietary LLMs, standard-prompt correctness ranged from 77.3% to 98.9%, with significant differences in 12 of 21 model pairs. On the 120 tasks solved by all 14 model-prompt configurations, no model performed best across all selected code properties. A security-focused prompt produced no significant change in correctness or consistent reduction in the selected static-analysis findings, while increasing program length and cyclomatic complexity across all models. These findings show that reliable evaluation of LLM-generated code requires validated ground truth, protected tests, and multiple explicitly interpreted measures. CodeAssay provides a reproducible basis for evidence-based model evaluation in AI-augmented software development.

</details>


### [150] [EffiHolmes: Differential Profiling-Guided Repository Level Time Inefficiency Fix Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03558)
*Haowen Yang,Yun Peng,Zishuo Ding*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EffiHolmes框架，用于仓库级别的软件时间效率缺陷修复定位。该方法通过默认和缩放工作负载下的差分剖析识别低效热点，提取连接热点与报告函数的紧凑执行路径，并利用领域引导的LLM推理定位底层低效逻辑。同时引入首个仓库级低效定位基准RepoEffi-Bench（含140个高质量问题），实验表明EffiHolmes在多项指标上优于现有最先进基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型软件系统常存在时间效率问题导致执行时间过长，但其定位比功能缺陷更困难——既不产生测试失败也无堆栈线索，传统和基于LLM的缺陷定位方法均不适用。运行剖析虽提供替代证据，但在仓库级场景面临三大挑战：单次剖析无法区分低效热点与执行噪声、现有剖析器难以从大量后台执行中提取相关执行路径、观察到的热点与实际修复位置之间存在语义鸿沟。

Method: EffiHolmes采用三步方法：(1) 在默认和缩放工作负载下进行差分剖析，识别低效热点；(2) 提取连接热点与报告的效率低下函数之间的紧凑执行路径；(3) 使用领域引导的LLM推理来定位底层低效逻辑。同时构建RepoEffi-Bench基准数据集，从流行Python仓库收集140个高质量效率问题。

Result: 实验表明EffiHolmes一致性地优于最先进的基于检索、智能体和剖析的基线方法。在GPT-5.1上文件级Acc@3提升4.29个百分点，在qwen3-4b上函数级Acc@5提升15.00个百分点，且在不同模型容量下保持稳健。

Conclusion: EffiHolmes通过结合差分剖析、路径提取与LLM推理，有效解决了仓库级时间效率缺陷定位的难题，为效率问题定位提供了首个基准和新颖的框架方案，并展现出跨模型容量的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large software systems often suffer from time inefficiencies that cause excessive execution time despite functional correctness. Localizing their fix locations is difficult because, unlike functional bugs, they produce neither test failures nor stack-trace clues, making traditional and recent LLM-based fault localization methods unsuitable. Runtime profiling provides alternative evidence but faces three challenges in repository-level settings: single-run profiling cannot reliably distinguish inefficiency hotspots from execution noise; existing profilers struggle to extract relevant execution paths from extensive background execution; and a semantic gap remains between observed hotspots and actual fix locations. We propose EffiHolmes, an LLM-based framework for repository-level time inefficiency fix localization. EffiHolmes uses differential profiling under default and scaled workloads to identify inefficiency hotspots, extracts compact execution paths connecting these hotspots to the reported inefficient function, and employs domain-guided LLM reasoning to locate the underlying inefficiency logic. We also introduce RepoEffi-Bench, the first benchmark for repository-level inefficiency localization, containing 140 high-quality issues collected from popular Python repositories. Experiments show that EffiHolmes consistently outperforms state-of-the-art retrieval-, agent-, and profiling-based baselines, improving file-level Acc@3 by 4.29 percentage points with GPT-5.1 and function-level Acc@5 by 15.00 percentage points with qwen3-4b. It also remains robust across model capacities.

</details>


### [151] [From Bug Reports to Browser-Executable Procedures: An LLM-Driven Agent for Web GUI Bug Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03598)
*Cunming Zhang,Yu Pei,Michail Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ReBug，一个上下文感知的智能体系统，能够从自然语言web GUI bug报告中重建、执行和验证浏览器级别的复现流程，通过在真实浏览器中驱动操作来实现端到端的视觉执行与验证。在667个真实bug报告上，ReBug平均RSR达49.96%，任务完成率74.96%，动作执行成功率86.54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自然语言bug报告中复现web GUI缺陷对软件维护至关重要，但现有方法主要针对代码单元或移动应用，缺乏对web GUI的端到端视觉执行和验证能力，且报告常缺少依赖和输入文件等前置条件。

Method: ReBug将复现过程分为两个阶段：准备阶段重建缺失的前置条件并生成高层复现计划；执行阶段在浏览器中进行工具中介的交互操作，维护页面状态和动作历史的结构化摘要，并依据报告中的预期验证最终状态。

Result: 在来自四个开源web应用的667个真实bug报告上，ReBug在受控当前部署中优于两个基线，平均RSR为49.96%，平均任务完成率74.96%，平均动作执行成功率86.54%。历史回放显示成功的复现流程通常能在恢复的bug版本上暴露原始缺陷行为。

Conclusion: 显式的上下文重建和状态感知的浏览器执行能有效支持基于报告的浏览器复现，为web GUI bug自动复现提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Reproducing web GUI bugs from natural-language bug reports is critical for software maintenance, but remains difficult because reports often lack prerequisites such as dependencies and input files. Existing bug reproduction techniques mainly target code units or mobile applications and lack end-to-end visual execution and validation for web GUIs. We present ReBug, a context-aware agent system that reconstructs, executes, and validates browser-level reproduction procedures from web GUI bug reports by driving a real browser. ReBug separates reproduction into two stages. In the preparation stage, ReBug reconstructs missing prerequisites from the report and available artifacts, and it produces a high-level reproduction plan. In the execution stage, it performs tool-mediated interactions in the browser, maintains structured summaries of page state and action history, and validates the final state against expectations derived from the report. We evaluate ReBug on 667 real-world bug reports from four open-source web applications. On controlled current deployments, ReBug outperforms both baselines, achieving an average RSR of 49.96%, a mean task completion rate of 74.96%, and a mean action execution success rate of 86.54%. Our results show that explicit context reconstruction and state-aware browser execution effectively support report-derived browser reproduction, while historical replay shows that successful procedures often expose the original bug-present behavior on restored buggy versions.

</details>


### [152] [Pattern over Pixels: Measuring Pattern Completion Bias in Multimodal Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03691)
*Khai-Nguyen Nguyen,Oscar Chaparro,Antonio Mastropaolo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次提出视觉模式补全偏差基准测试，发现多模态大语言模型在将网页截图转换为前端代码时，会受到重复UI模式的强烈影响，产生模式一致但视觉错误的输出，所有五个前沿MLLM均表现出显著的偏差（均值偏差率高达69.78%-80.22%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在将网页截图转换为前端代码时，重复出现的UI模式可能导致模型产生视觉错误但模式一致性的输出，这一问题尚未被系统性研究。

Method: 基于Design2Code数据集中的30个网页，构建1,440张评估截图，覆盖结构卡片和文本样式模式，在标准及噪声叠加条件下进行掩码宽度或字号值的补全任务，评估五个前沿MLLM，并分析推理努力与偏差的关系。

Result: 卡片宽度扰动平均偏差率69.78%，文本字号扰动偏差率80.22%。卡片平均准确率仅21.17%，文本仅7.89%。Codex-5.3表现最佳，但文本任务准确率从卡片的68.61%骤降至13.89%。Flash-3.0在文本上达到96.11%的最大偏差率。噪声、微弱扰动和边界位置会进一步增加偏差。

Conclusion: 多模态代码生成中存在一种具体的失败模式（视觉模式补全偏差），其严重程度与视觉显著性密切相关。推理努力增加与更低偏差相关，但定性证据表明即使模型能识别异常元素，仍可能覆盖模式一致的答案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to translate webpage screenshots into front-end code, but repeated UI patterns may sway them toward visually incorrect yet pattern-consistent outputs. In this work, we test how repeated webpage patterns hurt MLLM accuracy on an objective screenshot-to-code fill-in-the-blank task. We introduce the first benchmark for visual pattern-completion bias, where one localized element in a repeated UI pattern is perturbed and the model must recover the masked width or font-size value from the screenshot and HTML context. Starting from 30 webpages curated from the Design2Code dataset, we build 1,440 evaluated screenshots spanning structural card and text-style patterns under standard and noise-overlaid conditions. We evaluate five frontier MLLMs and find that all are strongly biased toward the repeated baseline. Mean bias rate reaches 69.78% on card-width perturbations and 80.22% on text font-size perturbations, while mean accuracy is only 21.17% and 7.89%, respectively. Codex-5.3 performs best but still drops from 68.61% accuracy on cards to 13.89% on text, while Flash-3.0 reaches 96.11% bias on text. Noise, subtler perturbations, and boundary positions further increase bias rate. Reasoning analysis further shows that greater reasoning effort correlates with lower bias, yet qualitative evidence reveals that models can identify the anomalous element and still override it with the pattern-consistent answer. Our results identify a concrete failure mode in multimodal code generation and show that its severity is strongly associated with visual saliency

</details>


### [153] [We Must Have Missed This Comment: Detecting and Repairing Stale Function References in Linux Kernel Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03734)
*Kexin Sun,Yunbo Lyu,Xutong Ma,Hongyu Kuang,Ratnadira Widyasari,He Zhang,Xiaoxing Ma,Julia Lawall,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ReCite方法，用于检测和修复Linux内核代码注释中过时的函数引用。通过三阶段流程（检测未解析的函数形式符号、追踪Git历史演变、生成基于LLM的修复建议），在Linux内核v6.18-rc1上检测到869个过时引用，其中89.0%的修复建议具有指导价值，75个提交的补丁中有50个已被接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linux内核不断演化，代码注释中的函数引用可能因函数被重构或删除而过时，这些过时的引用会误导维护者并阻碍代码理解。先前的工作主要关注Javadoc注释与其直接注释函数之间的语义错位，无法处理Linux内核中这种外部引起的过时引用问题。

Method: ReCite采用三阶段方法：(1) 检测未解析的函数形式符号——注释中看似引用函数但当前代码库中找不到匹配函数的符号；(2) 通过Git历史追踪每个未解析符号的演化过程；(3) 基于演化历史和当前代码上下文生成基于LLM的修复建议。

Result: 在Linux内核v6.18-rc1上，ReCite检测到869个过时引用并生成修复建议。对200个抽样修复的人工评估显示，178个（89.0%）提供有用的修复指导，其中85个（42.5%）可直接应用。提交的75个补丁中有50个已被接受。

Conclusion: ReCite有效解决了Linux内核中代码注释因函数演化而过时的引用问题，其修复建议质量高且实际接受率高，证明了该方法在真实世界大型代码库中的实用价值。

Abstract: As the Linux kernel evolves, code comments may become outdated, as the functions they reference can be refactored or removed independently without corresponding updates to the comments. Such stale function references can mislead maintainers and thus hinder code comprehension. Prior work on detecting code-comment inconsistency mainly focused on addressing semantic misalignment between Javadoc comments and their directly annotated functions, making them inapplicable to this type of externally induced staleness in the Linux kernel. Therefore, we propose ReCite, a three-stage approach to identify and repair such stale references: (1) detecting unresolved function-form symbols -- symbols in comments that appear to reference functions but for which no matching function can be found in the current codebase, (2) tracing the evolution history of each unresolved symbol through the Git history, and (3) generating LLM-based repair suggestions grounded in the evolution history and current code context. On Linux kernel v6.18-rc1, ReCite detects 869 stale references with generated repair suggestions. A manual evaluation on 200 sampled repairs shows that 178 (89.0%) provide useful repair guidance, with 85 (42.5%) directly applicable. Of our 75 submitted patches, 50 have been accepted. We also empirically study all unresolved function-form symbols.

</details>


### [154] [Can LLMs Test Terminal User Interfaces?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03743)
*Chao Peng,Ruida Hu,Ajitha Rajan,Tegawendé F Bissyandé,Jacques Klein,Cuiyun Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对终端用户界面（TUI）应用缺乏专门测试方法的问题，调研了197个真实TUI应用后发现测试覆盖率极低，构建了一个跨四种语言框架的无头基准测试平台，对比了四个前沿LLM与随机探索的测试效果，发现随机测试在时间预算下是强基线，LLM虽单次交互更高效但无模型全面占优，且行覆盖率不能可靠预测崩溃发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TUI应用在开发者工具中已非常普遍，但缺乏专门的测试方法论。现有研究表明测试代码很少真正交互到TUI界面，这暴露出巨大的测试缺口，亟需系统化的研究和基准来推动TUI测试的发展。

Method: 首先调研197个真实TUI应用以了解测试现状；将应用封装为跨ratatui/Rust、bubbletea/Go、textual/Python和ink/TypeScript四种框架的无头基准，打包为带插桩的Docker镜像；记录行覆盖、组件覆盖、渲染终端状态及崩溃；在相等壁钟时间预算下比较四个前沿LLM与随机探索，并引入自动推导启动输入的方法。

Result: 发现仅12%的测试代码实际触达界面，45%的界面测试从不发送输入；没有LLM在所有指标上全面占优；随机探索因更高的吞吐量而在崩溃发现上占优，但LLM在单次交互上更高效且能触及输入门控故障；自动推导启动输入带来最大实际收益；行覆盖率与崩溃发现相关性弱。

Conclusion: 自动化TUI测试是可行的但远未解决，模型选择不如诚实的基线重要；行覆盖率不宜作为测试有效性的代理指标；发布tuicov和tuibot两个工具供社区使用和进一步研究。

Abstract: Terminal User Interfaces (TUIs) combine the stateful, screen-oriented behaviour of GUIs with terminal deployment and are now common in developer tools. Yet they lack a dedicated testing methodology. We survey 197 real-world TUI applications: only 12% of test code exercises the interface, and 45% of those tests never send input, checking a static frame instead. We turn these applications into a headless benchmark spanning ratatui/Rust, bubbletea/Go, textual/Python, and ink/TypeScript, packaging each as an instrumented Docker image. We record line and widget coverage where reliable, rendered terminal states, and crashes. Under equal wall-clock budgets, we compare four frontier LLMs with random exploration. No model dominates. Random is a strong time-budgeted baseline, but its crash advantage comes from higher throughput: per interaction, LLM guidance is more efficient and uniquely reaches input-gated faults. Automatically deriving launch inputs yields the largest practical gain, enabling applications that otherwise never start. Line coverage poorly predicts crash discovery, weakening it as a proxy for test effectiveness. Automated TUI testing is feasible but far from solved, and honest baselines matter more than model choice. We release the coverage tool tuicov at https://github.com/tui-testing/tuicov and the testing framework tuibot at https://github.com/tui-testing/tuibot.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Separating Intelligence from Inference: A Standard for Edge-Native AI Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02608)
*Venkat Vinjam,Krishnaiah Narukulla*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文指出当前AI产业将模型训练与推理混用同一集中式基础设施存在根本性架构低效，提出"分离原则"——训练集中、推理边缘化，并量化了边缘推理在能源与碳排放上的巨大节省，同时提出了两类新硬件设备及八组件参考架构栈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI产业已投入3000亿美元建设集中式数据中心，但大语言模型推理这一工作负载在架构上并不需要集中化。将不可约集中化的训练与天然可并行化的推理混用同一物理硬件，是当代AI基础设施的核心架构低效，亟需重新设计。

Method: 提出分离原则：智能集中训练后以软件形式分发，推理在靠近数据源的边缘硬件上执行。量化十亿日活用户的能源影响，定义两类新设备（个人AI计算机PAC和企业AI工作站CAW），并设计包含八个组件的参考架构栈（权重分发、主权感知路由、热自适应量化、多租户资源管理、联邦网络推理、加密溯源、隐私保护遥测、分布式上下文窗口扩展）。

Result: 研究表明，完全边缘驻留的推理架构相对于当前集中式实践，每年可节省约19太瓦时（TWh）能源和730万吨二氧化碳排放。

Conclusion: 边缘推理架构在文明尺度上具有显著的能源与环境效益，部分架构组件已有待批美国专利申请，作者将其作为候选开放架构原则提出。

Abstract: The artificial intelligence industry has constructed a USD 300 billion centralized data center infrastructure to serve a workload, large language model inference, that does not architecturally require centralization. This paper articulates the central architectural inefficiency of contemporary AI infrastructure: the conflation of model training (irreducibly centralized, capital-intensive, one-time per model version) with model inference (parallelizable, latency-sensitive, recurring per query) on the same physical hardware. We propose the separation principle: intelligence is trained centrally and shipped as software; inference executes on hardware near the data source, at the edge. We quantify the energy implications at civilizational scale and show that a fully edge-resident inference architecture for one billion daily users saves approximately 19 TWh per year and 7.3 megatons of CO2 annually relative to current centralized practice. We specify two new device classes, the Personal AI Computer (PAC) and the Corporate AI Workstation (CAW), with concrete hardware tiers, memory bandwidth requirements, thermal envelopes, and software interfaces. We then describe a reference architectural stack of eight components addressing weight distribution, sovereignty-aware routing, thermal-adaptive quantization, multi-tenant resource management, federated network inference, cryptographic provenance, privacy-preserving telemetry, and distributed context window extension. Several components are the subject of pending United States patent applications by the first author and are presented here as candidate open architectural principles

</details>


### [156] [KernelBrain: Coarse-to-Fine, Budget-Aware Search for Agentic GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02611)
*Shuai Che,Gang Peng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种实用的GPU内核优化代理，结合LLM引导的变异、自适应资源分配、策略门控评估和性能分析器辅助诊断，在Triton内核生成任务中实现最高6.72倍于PyTorch、1.4倍于SOTA代理的加速，同时降低48%的优化时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化GPU内核优化在实践中仍面临挑战：生成的变体可能违反正确性约束、运行时测量存在噪声、搜索过程容易过早停滞，需要一个更实用的优化代理来解决这些问题。

Method: 设计了四部分组成的优化代理系统：(1) LLM引导的变异生成候选内核；(2) 自适应资源分配机制；(3) 策略门控评估用于筛选候选；(4) 性能分析器辅助诊断来指导优化方向。系统先用低成本评估筛选大量候选，仅对高质量的候选分配高保真度预算进行深入优化和演进。

Result: 在Triton内核生成任务上，相比PyTorch实现0.88x-6.72x加速，相比SOTA内核代理最多1.4x加速，优化时间最多降低48%。

Conclusion: 通过多级评估策略和LLM驱动的方法，该设计在保证正确性的前提下同时提升了内核质量和搜索效率，证明了一种实用的工业级GPU内核自动优化路径。

Abstract: Automating GPU kernel optimization remains difficult in practice: generated variants can violate correctness constraints, runtime measurements are noisy, and search often stalls early. We present a practical optimization agent that combines LLM-guided mutation, adaptive resource allocation, policy-gated evaluation, and profiler-informed diagnosis. The system screens many candidates with low-cost evaluation and allocates higher-fidelity budget only to promising survivors to optimize and evolve GPU kernels. On important Triton kernel generation tasks, this design improves both kernel quality and search efficiency, reaching 0.88x-6.72x speedup over PyTorch and up to 1.4x speedup over the state-of-the-art kernel agent, with up to 48% lower optimization time.

</details>


### [157] [Axiomatic shared-medium coordination for stigmergic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02619)
*Fernando Paredes García*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种与媒介无关的stigmergic协调比较框架，以抽象"使能-响应签名"为比较对象，证明了粗商它在响应充分性条件下的充要条件，并给出了响应充分性修复的典范构造及动态侧的单步与有限串行迹提升结果，最终在元组空间和时间戳虚拟stigmergic数据空间上进行了实例化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stigmergic协调（stigmergic coordination）在不同的媒介（medium）中有着各自特有的形式化，缺乏一个跨媒介的统一比较框架，使得不同媒介上的协调行为难以进行系统性的对比与分析。

Method: 本文构建了一个媒介无关的比较层，以抽象"使能-响应签名"（abstract enabled-response signature）作为比较对象。在元数据细化方面，证明了粗商在响应充分性上成立当且仅当每个商纤维是响应对齐的；当不成立时，给出典范的最粗响应充分性修复，并证明了任意修复必须保留对应的纤维级响应类数量。在动态方面，提出了商兼容匹配写保持单步行为、新鲜度条件动作产生单步不可提升障碍，以及匹配单步结果可提升到有限串行迹等结果，并在元组空间和时间戳虚拟stigmergic数据空间上进行了实例化。

Result: 论文得到了一个完整的"正/负"定理循环：一方面给出了粗商响应充分性的充要条件（正定理），另一方面当条件不成立时给出了最粗响应充分性修复的典范构造及下界约束（负定理）。动态侧则获得了单步行为保持、不可提升性障碍以及有限串行迹提升等结果。

Conclusion: 该工作形成了一个媒介无关的stigmergic协调比较框架，并附带了一个显式的正/负定理循环；更丰富的路径级和并发语义不在当前研究范围内，留待未来工作。

Abstract: Stigmergic coordination has many medium-specific formalizations. This paper formulates a medium-agnostic comparison layer whose comparison object is the abstract enabled-response signature. For metadata refinements, we prove that a coarse quotient is response-adequate exactly when each quotient fiber is response-aligned. When that fails, there is a canonical coarsest response-adequate repair, and every response-adequate repair must retain at least the corresponding fiberwise number of response classes. On the dynamic side, quotient-compatible matched writes preserve one-step behavior, freshness-conditional actions yield a one-step non-liftability obstruction, and the matched one-step result lifts to finite serial traces. These results are instantiated for a tuple-space medium and a timestamped virtual-stigmergic dataspace. The result is a medium-agnostic comparison framework with one explicit positive/negative theorem cycle; richer pathwise and concurrent semantics remain outside scope.

</details>


### [158] [Configurable and Hierarchical Allreduce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02884)
*Valentino Guerrini,Ke Fan,Sidharth Kumar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CHIARA是一种可配置的分层Allreduce算法，通过逻辑批次-通道拓扑编码硬件层级，采用受限活跃数据块的分阶段调度，并使用旋转根通道原语分散跨批次通信，在Polaris、Aurora和Fugaku上相比厂商MPI_Allreduce分别获得最高1.94x、13.43x和13.48x加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPI_Allreduce是大规模科学计算和分布式机器学习中性能最关键的集合操作之一，但在小到中等消息规模下仍面临挑战：延迟、同步深度以及域内和域间通信间的强硬件层级差异都会增加单次调用成本。

Method: 提出CHIARA，一种可配置的分层Allreduce方案。它通过逻辑批次-通道拓扑编码硬件层级，执行分阶段调度使每次仅有部分规约向量处于活跃状态。跨批次通信通过旋转根通道原语分布到多个秩上，避免集中式领导者。此外，通过保留Reduce-Scatter/Allgather边界上的通道对齐中间布局，支持半组合的Rabenseifner风格Allreduce，消除冗余的域内重组。

Result: 在Polaris、Aurora和Fugaku上评估，相比厂商MPI_Allreduce分别获得最高1.94x、13.43x和13.48x加速比，在并行k-means应用中实现最高2.2x的端到端加速。

Conclusion: CHIARA通过硬件层级感知的调度和去中心化的通信设计，有效解决了中小消息规模下Allreduce的性能瓶颈，并在不同超级计算机平台上展示了显著的性能提升，证明了其在真实应用中的实用价值。

Abstract: MPI_Allreduce is among the most performance-critical collectives in large-scale scientific computing and distributed machine learning, yet the small- and medium-message regime remains challenging: latency, synchronization depth, and strong hardware hierarchy between intra- and inter-domain communication all compound per-invocation cost. We present CHIARA, a configurable hierarchical Allreduce that encodes hardware hierarchy through a logical batch-lane topology and executes a staged schedule in which only a bounded portion of the reduction vector is active at a time. Inter-batch communication is distributed across multiple ranks via a rotating-root lane primitive, avoiding centralized leaders. Tool further enables a semi-composed Rabenseifner-style Allreduce by preserving a lane-aligned intermediate layout across the Reduce-Scatter/Allgather boundary, eliminating redundant intra-domain reorganization. We evaluate Tool on Polaris, Aurora, and Fugaku, achieving speedups of up to 1.94x, 13.43x, and 13.48x over vendor MPI_Allreduce, and up to 2.2x end-to-end speedup in a parallel k-means application.

</details>


### [159] [Distributed Algorithms for Near-Equitable Coloring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02910)
*Amit Nir,David Peleg*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究分布式网络中近似均衡图着色问题，提出了一系列快速随机分布式算法，在颜色调色板大小（Δ+1）和颜色频率均匀性（接近σ）这两个冲突参数之间探索权衡，并在顺序、CONGEST和Congested Clique模型中分析了算法的时间复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hajnal-Szemerédi定理保证每个n顶点最大度Δ、直径D的图都存在均衡(Δ+1)-着色，且已知O(n²Δ)时间的顺序算法。然而在分布式网络中，如何在少量轮次内计算颜色频率接近σ的着色，同时保持接近Δ+1的调色板大小，是一个尚未充分探索的问题。

Method: 作者提出了一系列快速随机分布式算法，这些算法代表了两难权衡（调色板大小与颜色频率均匀性）曲线上的不同点，并在顺序、CONGEST和Congested Clique（CC）模型下对算法性质进行理论分析和复杂度研究。

Result: 揭示了这两个冲突参数（调色板大小与颜色频率均匀性）之间存在权衡关系，并给出了一系列在不同权衡点上运作的分布式算法及其在三种计算模型中的时间复杂度和近似性质分析。

Conclusion: 分布式环境中实现近似均衡着色需要牺牲调色板大小或颜色均匀性，两者之间存在本质性权衡；所提出的算法套件为该权衡提供了多种可调节的实现方案。

Abstract: For an $n$-vertex graph of maximum degree $Δ$ and diameter $D$, an equitable $(Δ+1)$-coloring is a vertex coloring where the frequency of each color (namely, the number of vertices it colors) are all equal to $σ=n/(Δ+1)$ (up to rounding). The Hajnal-Szemerédi Theorem guarantees the existence of such a coloring for every graph, and an $O(n^2Δ)$ time sequential algorithm is known for computing such a coloring. Here, we study near-equitable graph coloring in distributed networks. The main question of interest is how close one can remain to the desired palette size of $Δ+1$ while computing, in few distributed rounds, a coloring whose frequencies are close to $σ$. It appears that these two conflicting parameters exhibit a tradeoff, which we attempt to explore. We present a suite of fast randomized distributed algorithms representing varying points on this tradeoff, analyze their properties, and study their time complexity in the sequential, CONGEST and Congested Clique (CC) models.

</details>


### [160] [Diameter-Free Distributed Frequency Control for Graph Coloring in the CONGEST Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02920)
*Amit Nir,David Peleg*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出两种随机适当着色算法，在同步CONGEST模型中控制颜色频率，无需支付依赖于网络直径的协调成本。两种算法均以高概率运行O((λ+1)log n)轮，分别实现颜色频率的两侧相对均衡和单侧上限控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式图着色中，传统的适当着色算法往往只保证相邻节点颜色不同，而不控制每种颜色的使用频率，这可能导致颜色分布严重不均。已有方法通常需要依赖网络直径的协调成本来实现频率控制。本文旨在设计不依赖网络直径的随机着色算法，在保证适当着色的同时有效控制颜色频率。

Method: 本文提出两种随机化适当着色算法。第一种算法使用χ=⌈(2+δ)Δ⌉种颜色，以概率至少1-n^{-λ}保证每种颜色频率与n/χ的偏差为O_δ(√((λ+1)(n/χ)logn)+(λ+1)logn)，在显式负载条件下可实现两侧相对均衡。第二种算法适用于任意χ>Δ，使用Δ+⌈(Δ+1)/⌈ln n⌉⌉种颜色，对每种使用颜色的类大小实现O((λ+1)(σlog²n+logn))的单侧上限，其中σ=n/(Δ+1)。两种算法均在O((λ+1)logn)轮内完成。

Result: 第一种算法在固定δ>0下，以高概率(≥1-n^{-λ})实现颜色频率偏差的加性界限；第二种算法通过调色板宽松度χ-Δ控制颜色频率上限。两种算法的运行时间均为O((λ+1)logn)轮，与网络直径无关；第一种算法的时间常熟依赖δ。

Conclusion: 该工作表明在CONGEST模型中可以不依赖网络直径实现颜色频率的精细控制，为分布式适当着色中颜色负载均衡问题提供了新的高效解决方案。两种算法分别适用于不同场景：一种追求两侧均衡的颜色分布，另一种在调色板接近Δ时仍能提供单侧频率上限保证。

Abstract: This paper presents two randomized proper-coloring algorithms that control color frequencies in the synchronous CONGEST model without paying a diameter-dependent coordination cost. Let $λ\geq 1$ denote the desired failure exponent. For every fixed $δ> 0$, the first algorithm uses $χ= \lceil (2+δ)Δ\rceil$ colors and, with probability at least $1 - n^{-λ}$, outputs a proper coloring that bounds the deviation of every color frequency from $n/χ$ by $O_δ(\sqrt{(λ+1)(n/χ)\lg n} + (λ+1)\lg n)$. Under an explicit load condition, this additive guarantee yields two-sided relative balance. The second algorithm works with every $χ> Δ$ and gives a one-sided frequency cap controlled by the palette slack $χ- Δ$. In particular, it uses $Δ+ \lceil (Δ+1)/\lceil \ln n \rceil \rceil$ colors and caps every used color class by $O((λ+1)(σ\lg^2 n + \lg n))$, where $σ= n/(Δ+1)$. Both algorithms run in $O((λ+1)\lg n)$ rounds, with no dependence on the network diameter; for the first algorithm, the multiplicative constant in the time bound depends on $δ$.

</details>


### [161] [Pruning-Aware Multi-Cluster Co-Inference for Large AI Models in AI-RANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03026)
*Xiaowen Cao,Zhonghao Lyu,Shicheng Chu,Zezhong Zhang,Dingzhu Wen,Guangxu Zhu,Kaibin Huang,Shuguang Cui,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多集群LAIM协同推理框架，边缘服务器协调多个用户集群协作执行推理任务，并通过联合优化模型剪枝率、任务调度、带宽分配和传输功率来最小化推理失真，仿真结果表明该框架显著优于现有基准方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模AI模型的规模和计算需求日益增长，在资源受限的分布式环境中实现高效推理面临重大挑战，需要新的协同推理框架来应对。

Method: 提出多集群LAIM协同推理框架，集群内设备用轻量级LAIM提取局部特征并传输至边缘服务器进行聚合融合；利用率失真理论和部分信息分解建立理论框架刻画模型剪枝与协同推理性能间的权衡；在此基础上构建联合优化问题，优化模型剪枝率、任务调度策略、带宽分配和传输功率，目标是最小化推理失真同时满足时延、能耗和服务器容量约束。

Result: 大量仿真结果表明，所提出的框架在推理准确性和资源效率方面显著优于现有基准方案，在多集群边缘智能网络中表现出卓越性能。

Conclusion: 该多集群协同推理框架能够有效解决大规模AI模型在资源受限分布式环境中的高效推理问题，通过联合优化各资源维度实现了推理精度与资源效率的平衡。

Abstract: The increasing scale and computational demands of large artificial intelligence models (LAIMs) present significant challenges for efficient inference in resource-constrained distributed environments. In this paper, we propose a multi-cluster LAIM co-inference framework, where an edge server equipped with multiple graphics processing units (GPUs) coordinates multiple user clusters to execute inference tasks collaboratively. Within each cluster, devices capture data from diverse perspectives and employ lightweight on-device LAIMs to extract local features. These features are then transmitted to the edge server, where they are aggregated and fused to generate a more accurate inference outcome. To reveal the fundamental trade-off between model pruning and collaborative inference performance, we develop a theoretical framework that characterizes the impact of pruning ratios and device contributions using rate-distortion theory and partial information decomposition. Based on this analysis, we formulate a joint optimization problem that determines the model pruning ratio, the task scheduling strategy, the bandwidth allocation, and the transmission power, with the goal of minimizing the inference distortion while satisfying the constraints of latency, energy consumption, and server capacity. Extensive simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework significantly outperforms existing benchmark schemes, achieving superior inference accuracy and resource efficiency in multi-cluster edge intelligence networks.

</details>


### [162] [Trust-Aware Topology Learning for Dynamic Decentralized Federated Learning under Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03156)
*Shubham Vaishnav,Murtaza Rangwala,Ali Beikmohammadi,Sindri Magnússon,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DMTT是一种基于MURMURA框架的去中心化个性化联邦学习协议，通过证据深度学习、拓扑信任筛选和链接可靠性建模，在动态移动网络拓扑操纵攻击下维持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态移动去中心化联邦学习中，攻击者可以同时毒化模型更新和设备用于选择协作者的拓扑信息，现有鲁棒聚合方法无法在拓扑操纵攻击下保持性能。

Method: 在MURMURA基础上扩展至时变图，设备维护置信加权的局部拓扑视图，结合链接可靠性估计、签名拓扑声明、证人证实和Beta分布源信任模型，通过融合模型兼容性、拓扑信任和链接可靠性的复合评分筛选可信协作者集合进行聚合。

Result: 在UCI HAR和PAMAP2数据集上，面对10%-80%攻击者比例，诚实节点精度分别维持在0.862和0.829以上；静态和动态FedAvg完全崩溃，Krum等鲁棒聚合器不及本地基线，DMTT是唯一在所有比例下超越基线的方案，且拜占庭聚合权重为零。

Conclusion: DMTT通过理论证明筛选混合矩阵将拜占庭影响限制在可消失的残差内，并在真实节点端到端验证了其在拓扑操纵攻击下的鲁棒性和实用性。

Abstract: In dynamic mobile decentralized federated learning (DFL), adversaries can poison both model updates and the topology information devices use to choose collaborators. We present DMTT (Dynamic MURMURA with Trusted Topology), a decentralized personalized FL protocol built on MURMURA, which uses evidential deep learning to down-weight distribution-mismatched peers, extended here to time-varying graphs under topology-manipulation attacks. Each device maintains a confidence-weighted local topology view from link-reliability estimates, signed topology claims, witness corroboration, and a Beta-distributed source-trust model, then aggregates only over a trust-screened collaborator set using a composite score fusing model compatibility, topology trust, and link reliability. We prove the screened mixing matrices confine Byzantine influence to a bounded residual $δ_{max}$ that vanishes under perfect screening, and implement DMTT as a coordinator-free distributed system with each client running as an independent ZeroMQ process synchronized by a shared wall-clock epoch. On UCI HAR and PAMAP2, each partitioned across 100 mobile clients with Dirichlet heterogeneity, DMTT sustains honest-node accuracy above 0.862 (UCI HAR) and 0.829 (PAMAP2) across all tested adversary fractions (10 to 80%), nearly matching no-attack accuracy at low fractions and degrading gracefully toward local-only performance at extremes; static and dynamic FedAvg collapse to chance at every fraction, and robust aggregators (Krum, BALANCE, UBAR) fail to consistently beat a local-only baseline, while DMTT is the only method that clears this bar across both datasets at all fractions, with surviving Byzantine aggregation weight empirically zero throughout, consistent with $δ_{max}$=0. The protocol runs end-to-end on real nodes via a coordinator-free ZeroMQ backend on the Melbourne Research Cloud.

</details>


### [163] [LPV Control for Dynamic Power Capping in High-Performance Computing under Mixed Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03367)
*Mohamed Abdeldjalil Maziz,Kouds Halitim,Bogdan Robu,Sophie Cerf*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了HPC系统中面向混合工作负载场景的动态功耗调节问题，对比了增益调度PI控制器和基于H∞控制的线性参数变化(LPV)控制器两种反馈策略的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPC系统在能耗与性能之间需要平衡，而静态功耗封顶机制（如Intel RAPL）缺乏对动态变化工作负载的适应性，需要研究更灵活的动态功耗调节方案。

Method: 研究了两种反馈控制策略：（1）增益调度比例积分（PI）控制器；（2）通过H∞控制设计的多面体线性参数变化（LPV）控制器。两者均由在内存阶段和计算阶段之间切换的工作负载指示器进行调度，并在实际功耗封顶约束下评估跟踪性能、相切换和鲁棒性。

Result: 两种控制器均能满足功耗限制要求，但LPV控制器在跟踪误差、控制方差和相切换过程中的瞬态平滑性方面均优于增益调度PI控制器。

Conclusion: 在混合工作负载场景下，LPV控制器相比增益调度PI控制器具有更好的动态功耗调节性能，能够实现更低跟踪误差和更平滑的相切换响应。

Abstract: Balancing energy consumption and performance remains a critical challenge in High Performance Computing (HPC) systems. While static power capping mechanisms such as Intel's Running Average Power Limit (RAPL) offer basic control capabilities, they lack the flexibility to adapt to dynamically varying workloads. This work studies dynamic power regulation for mixed workload scenarios. We investigate two feedback strategies: a gain scheduled proportional-integral (PI) controller and a polytopic linear parameter-varying (LPV) controller synthesized via H$\infty$ control, both scheduled by a workload indicator that changes between memory and compute phase. We evaluate tracking performance, phase switching, and robustness under practical power cap constraints. While both controllers respect power limits, the LPV design achieves lower tracking error, lower control variance, and smoother transients during phase changes than gain scheduled PI.

</details>


### [164] [FedRings: A Scalable and Topology-Aware Federated Learning Framework for LEO Satellite Constellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03436)
*Ziwu Liu,Inês Pinto Gouveia,Rehana Yasmin,Paulo Esteves-Verissimo,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FedRings是一个面向低地球轨道（LEO）卫星网络的去中心化联邦学习框架，通过环状通信结构、时空路由策略、链路感知通信调度和自适应稀疏增量聚合，解决LEO网络动态拓扑下的训练效率问题，并在真实场景中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星网络频繁的链路变化、短接触时间和高度动态的拓扑结构，使得集中式或同步训练效率低下且难以扩展，亟需一种适配动态卫星网络的去中心化联邦学习方案。

Method: 提出FedRings框架：1) 将卫星组织为环状通信结构；2) 采用时空路由策略结合链路感知通信调度，使模型交换与实际可见窗口和时变连通模式对齐；3) 沿环传播模型更新，使用自适应稀疏增量聚合渐进合并和压缩更新以降低通信开销；4) 引入历史补偿机制以维持通信中断情况下的训练连续性。

Result: 实验表明，FedRings在真实LEO网络场景中持续优于现有方法，同时显著降低通信成本，实现了动态网络下的稳定高效学习。

Conclusion: 通过结合拓扑感知路由、通信调度和高效聚合，FedRings能够在高度动态的LEO卫星网络中实现稳定高效的联邦学习，为卫星网络的分布式智能提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Federated learning over low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite networks is limited by frequent link changes, short contact times, and a highly dynamic topology, making centralized or synchronized training inefficient and hard to scale. To address this, we propose FedRings, a decentralized framework that organizes satellites into ring-based communication structures. It uses a spatio-temporal routing strategy with link-aware communication scheduling to align model exchange with actual visibility windows and time-varying connectivity patterns in LEO. Model updates are propagated along the ring using adaptive sparse incremental aggregation, which reduces communication overhead by progressively combining and compressing updates. To handle communication interruptions, a historical compensation mechanism maintains training continuity. By combining topology-aware routing, communication scheduling, and efficient aggregation, FedRings enables stable and efficient learning in dynamic LEO networks while reducing communication cost, and experiments show it consistently outperforms existing methods in realistic settings.

</details>


### [165] [TAOT: Topology-Aware Optimal Transport for Dynamic Expert Replica Placement in MoE Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03676)
*Lingyun Zhang,Henghua Zhang,Shilei Gu,Kai Mo,Shuai Han,Shiyong Li,Yanpeng Wang,Dou Shen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出TAOT，一种拓扑感知的最优传输方法，用于MoE训练中动态专家副本的放置。通过将热专家过载和轻载rank的空闲容量建模为带通信成本矩阵的熵正则化最优传输问题，利用Sinkhorn-Knopp迭代求解，结合整数副本匹配与token分配生成可执行调度，并实现guest权重传输与home专家计算的叠加隐藏通信开销。实验显示1.43倍端到端加速、平衡质量优于或持平SOTA，加权专家通信成本最多降低74%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE动态副本方法仅优化负载均衡，忽略了跨节点拓扑中移动专家权重的通信成本，导致跨节点通信开销可能抵消均衡收益，增加训练成本。

Method: 将热rank的过载和轻载rank的空闲容量建模为带通信成本矩阵的平衡熵正则化最优传输问题；用Sinkhorn-Knopp迭代求解得到rank级别的流提示；将整数副本匹配与token分配组合为可执行调度；在系统层面通过重叠guest权重传输与home专家计算来隐藏通信开销。

Result: 端到端MoE训练加速1.43倍；平衡质量达到或优于现有SOTA方法；在所有配置下获得最低的加权专家通信成本，最多降低74%。

Conclusion: TAOT通过在动态副本放置中同时建模负载均衡和拓扑通信成本，证明了两者可以联合优化，在不牺牲平衡质量的前提下大幅降低通信开销，提升了MoE训练的端到端效率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become a key architecture for scaling large language models (LLMs), yet its dynamic routing causes severe load imbalance in expert-parallel training. Existing dynamic-replica methods copy hot experts onto idle ranks to share computation, but they optimize load balance alone and ignore the cost of moving expert weights across a multi-node topology, so the resulting cross-node communication can outweigh the balancing gain and inflate training cost. We present TAOT, a topology-aware optimal transport method for dynamic expert-replica placement. TAOT models the overload on hot ranks and the spare capacity on lightly loaded ranks as a balanced entropy-regularized optimal transport problem with a communication-cost matrix, solves it with Sinkhorn-Knopp iterations to produce rank-level flow hints, and combines integer replica matching with token assignment into an executable schedule. At the system level, it overlaps guest-weight transfer with home-expert computation to hide the communication overhead. Experiments show TAOT achieves a 1.43x end-to-end MoE training speedup, reaches balance quality competitive with or better than existing state-of-the-art methods, and attains the lowest weighted expert-communication cost across all configurations, with up to a 74% reduction.

</details>


### [166] [Accelerating Dynamic Graph Clustering on GPU Architectures with cuGraph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03695)
*Nelson Aloysio Reis de Almeida Passos,Emanuele Carlini,Salvatore Trani*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GPU加速的时序网络社区检测框架，通过扩展谱聚类和基于模块度的算法（如Leiden贪心优化和Bethe-Hessian特征分解），在NVIDIA RAPIDS生态系统中实现多快照动态图的社区表征与追踪，最高可获得相比CPU参考实现约三个数量级的加速，并已作为开源软件发布，支持通过NetworkX-Temporal库的Python绑定实现零代码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态图上的社区检测算法无法直接有效处理时序网络，而现有的时序社区检测方法在计算效率上存在瓶颈，难以应对大规模快照式动态图的分析需求。

Method: 基于NVIDIA RAPIDS生态系统构建GPU加速框架，包含两条技术路线：一是支持多GPU的Leiden贪心优化（通过Dask实现工作负载分布式分配），二是对对称Bethe-Hessian算子进行特征分解；同时采用多层切片（multislice）模块度作为后端算法。

Result: 在等量工作预算下，多切片模块度后端相比CPU参考实现可达到约三个数量级的加速，加速效果取决于图密度和快照数量；在真实世界和合成数据集上验证了其适用性，并保持了与现有图分析管道的兼容性。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了时序网络中社区检测的计算效率问题，为流行病传播、金融系统、网络安全、轨迹与移动性分析等多个应用领域提供了高效的探索性分析工具，且以开源形式发布便于社区使用和扩展。

Abstract: This work addresses community detection in temporal networks through GPU-accelerated extensions of spectral clustering and modularity-based algorithms originally designed for static graphs. Built on the NVIDIA RAPIDS ecosystem, the framework enables the characterization and tracking of communities in snapshot-based dynamic graphs, either by Leiden greedy optimization with multi-GPU support via Dask-based workload distribution, or eigendecomposition of a symmetric Bethe-Hessian operator. Our multislice modularity backend achieves up to roughly three orders of magnitude speedup over the CPU reference under an equal-work budget, depending on graph density and snapshot count, while preserving compatibility with existing graph analytics pipelines. We demonstrate its applicability on real-world and synthetic datasets, facilitating exploratory analysis of structural network properties over time. Such capabilities are relevant across several application domains, such as epidemic spreading, financial systems, cybersecurity, and trajectory and mobility analysis. We release our implementation as free and open-source software, including Python bindings through the NetworkX-Temporal library for ease of use and zero-code acceleration with existing codebases.

</details>


### [167] [When Does Disaggregation Pay? Simulating Prefill--Decode--Attention--FFN Specialization for Agentic LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03741)
*Przemyslaw Forys,Haoran Wu,Can Xiao,Jiayi Nie,Tony Liu,Rika Antonova,Timothy Jones,Robert Mullins,Wayne Luk,Aaron Zhao,George A. Constantinides*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了HeteroPanacea，一个面向异质化智能体推理系统的跨栈仿真框架，支持推理阶段分解（prefill/decode）、量化和并行调度三个维度的系统级模拟，并发现Prefill-Decode分离可提升高达75%的吞吐量，四路PDAF分离在自定义NPU上表现最稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体推理（agentic inference）已成为LLM推理的主流范式，但其多轮交互和工具调用特性使prefill和decode阶段表现出截然不同的计算和内存带宽需求，单一同质GPU系统难以支撑，行业正转向异质化分离式服务系统（如Vera-Rubin平台结合GPU和Groq LPU），但各组件最优硬件配置仍未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出HeteroPanacea仿真框架，围绕三个维度进行系统级模拟：1) 分解式量化（disaggregated quantization）；2) 自动化的设备内与设备间并行调度；3) PDAF（prefill-decode-attention-FFN）NPU架构异质性。通过结合这三个维度提供跨栈仿真能力，并运行消融实验探究模型架构与分解收益之间的关系。

Result: 仿真验证了Prefill-Decode分离的收益，在现有GPU上相比传统伺服可提升高达75%的吞吐量；同时证明在假设自定义NPU的条件下，四路Prefill-Doctrine-Attention-FFN分离（PDAF）是跨不同模型提升吞吐量最一致的方案。

Conclusion: HeteroPanacea为未来异质化智能体推理系统提供了跨栈仿真工具，确认了推理阶段分解是提升吞吐量的有效手段，并揭示了更细粒度（PDAF四路）的分离架构在自定义NPU上的优势与一致性。

Abstract: Agentic inference now dominates the LLM inference landscape, requiring LLMs to actively engage in multi-turn interactions with tool-calling capabilities. This introduces a more complex workload for the underlying inference system: serving stages such as prefill and decode exhibit substantially different behaviors and demand distinct compute and memory-bandwidth capabilities. As a result, a single homogeneous GPU system now struggles to support agentic inference, motivating an industry shift toward heterogeneous systems with disaggregated serving capabilities, such as the emerging Vera-Rubin platform with GPUs and Groq LPUs. However, the question of what the optimal hardware should look like for each component in a heterogeneous system remains underexplored. To this end, we propose a novel simulation framework for disaggregated serving, termed \textbf{HeteroPanacea}, that enables system-level simulation across three dimensions: 1) disaggregated quantization, 2) automated intra- and inter-device parallelization scheduling, and 3) PDAF (prefill-decode-attention-FFN) NPU architectural heterogeneity. By combining these three axes, we provide a cross-stack simulation framework for future heterogeneous agentic serving systems. We confirm the benefit of Prefill Decode disaggregation, simulating increased serving throughput by up to 75\% compared to traditional serving with current GPUs and demonstrate 4 way Prefill Decode Attention FFN disaggregation is the most consistent for increasing throughput across different models, assuming custom NPUs. We also investigate the relationship between model architecture and gain from disaggregation by running a set of ablation studies.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [Caved or Convinced: Temporal Sampling Gates Claim Deference in Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03160)
*Yuxin Cao,Wei Song,Jingling Xue,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文研究了视频大语言模型在判断事件先后顺序时的两类失败：屈从于虚假主张或拒绝真实主张。作者将失败原因区分为"可用性"（采样帧是否包含两个事件）和"加权"（证据是否被信任），并通过保持主张不变的两种干预实验证明可用性是必要但非充分条件。最后提出反转测试，通过正反向评分采样帧来消除模型顺序先验，将顺序准确率提升至0.92-1.00。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频谄媚（sycophancy）研究只关注模型屈从虚假主张的问题，且通过教导模型减少信任用户来缓解，但这种方法在文本和图像模型中已被证明会恶化拒绝真实主张的问题。此外，两种失败在文献中被混为一谈，缺乏对根本原因的清晰区分。

Method: 作者设计了两个保持主张不变的干预：（1）帧保持重排（frame-preserving reorder），翻转主张的真实性；（2）采样偏移（sampling-offset shift），在固定帧预算下捕捉或错过两个事件。基于此提出反转测试（reversal test）：对采样帧进行正向和反向评分，抵消模型的顺序先验，然后根据结果回答、重新采样或弃权，全程不读取主张内容。

Result: 当事件被错过时，九种模型接受真假主张的比率相同（Youden's J=0）。九种模型中有五种能读取顺序，但其中四种仍然屈从于虚假主张，达到加权上限。反转测试将能读取顺序的模型的顺序准确率提升至0.92-1.00，对不能读取顺序的模型则选择弃权而非猜测。

Conclusion: 可用性是判断事件顺序的必要但非充分条件；信任无法在从未被采样的证据上进行校准。反转测试通过在采样帧层面消解模型先验，为视频模型的顺序判断提供了一个有效的校准方案。

Abstract: When asked which of two events came first, video large language models can fail in two opposite ways: cave to a false claim, or reject a true one. Prior video sycophancy work measures only the first and mitigates it by teaching the model to trust the user less, a fix known in text and image models to worsen the second. In video, both failures come from two causes the literature treats as one: availability, whether the sparse sampled frames contain the two events, and weighting, whether that evidence is trusted over the user. We separate them with two interventions that keep the claim fixed: a frame-preserving reorder that flips the claim's truth, and a sampling-offset shift that captures or misses both events at a fixed frame budget. When the events are missed, the two twins present identical frames, so each of the nine models we evaluate accepts a true and a false claim at the same rate, making Youden's $J=0$ by construction. Availability is necessary but not sufficient. Five of the nine read the order, yet four of those five still cave to the false claim, so their deference hits a weighting ceiling. Since trust cannot be calibrated over evidence that was never sampled, we propose a reversal test that cancels the model's order prior by scoring the sampled frames forward and reversed, then answers, resamples, or abstains without reading the claim. The test raises the order accuracy to 0.92-1.00 on the models that read the order and abstains rather than guesses on those that cannot.

</details>


### [169] [Hear to See: Discerning Stateful Listening for Audio-Visual Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03264)
*Leiye Liu,Miao Zhang,Jiahong Jiang,Jingjing Li,Jialong Zhong,Kai Peng,Tingwei Liu,Wei Ji,Yongri Piao,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出Hear to See (H2S)框架解决音视频实例分割中的两个核心挑战：混合音频与视觉实例的精确对应，以及音视频信号时间不对齐时的鲁棒跟踪。通过声学语义投影器(ASP)和异步动力学调制器(ADM)两个机制，在AVISeg数据集上实现了48.54 mAP的SOTA性能，较先前方法提升7.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频实例分割方法难以匹配重叠声学事件与视觉实例，且无法处理音视频信号的时间不同步问题。这两个关键挑战限制了模型在复杂场景下的分割精度与跟踪鲁棒性。

Method: H2S包含两个核心模块：(1) 声学语义投影器(ASP)用于分离混合音频，并建立从语义域到空间域的层次化对应关系；(2) 异步动力学调制器(ADM)通过音频调制的Mamba架构自适应调整状态转换，在动态变化期间优先处理当前信息，在稳定阶段保持连续性。

Result: 在AVISeg基准上，H2S使用COCO预训练的ResNet50骨干达到48.54 mAP，超越先前最佳方法7.8%，实现SOTA性能。

Conclusion: H2S通过解耦音频语义与调制异步时序状态，有效解决了重叠声源匹配和音视频不同步两大难题，验证了音频引导的实例分割方法的有效性。代码将在论文被接收后开源。

Abstract: Audio-visual instance segmentation (AVIS) requires accurately identifying and tracking individual sounding objects with pixel-level masks. Existing methods struggle to match overlapping acoustic events with visual instances and handle asynchronous audio-visual dynamics. Therefore, two critical questions arise: how can a model establish precise correspondence between overlapping sound sources and visual instances, and how can a model maintain robust tracking when audio and visual signals are temporally misaligned?This paper proposes Hear to See (H2S), addressing these challenges through two mechanisms. The Acoustic-Semantic Projector (ASP) disentangles mixed audio and establishes hierarchical correspondence from semantic to spatial domains. The Asynchronous Dynamics Modulator (ADM) adaptively adjusts state transitions via audio-modulated Mamba, prioritizing current information during dynamic variations and maintaining continuity in stable periods.Experiments on AVISeg show H2S achieves SOTA performance, attaining 48.54 mAP with a COCO pretrained ResNet50 and surpassing the previous by 7.8\%. The code will be open-sourced once the paper is accepted. The source code will be publicly available at https://github.com/leiyeliu/H2S.

</details>


### [170] [Balancing Efficiency and Efficacy: Training-Free Attention-Guided Switching Between Explicit and Latent Thoughts for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03450)
*Haoqian Kang,Liupeng Li,Kuofeng Gao,Jinpeng Wang,Zhenyu Lu,Bin Chen,Ke Chen,Yaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的推理策略AGS（Attention-Guided Switching），通过视觉-文本注意力比率这一新指标动态解耦多模态大语言模型中的感知与推理过程，在不增加训练成本的情况下同时提升准确率和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型推理方法存在两大问题：显式文本CoT计算开销大且易产生视觉幻觉；现有的潜在推理方法需要昂贵的训练。此外，直接在多模态场景下适配训练无关的LLM推理机制性能不稳定，其根本原因在于这类方法依赖token级熵，混淆了感知模糊性（如视觉细节不清）和逻辑不确定性（如推理步骤复杂）两类本质不同的不确定性。

Method: 提出一种新的指标——视觉到文本注意力比率（vision-to-text attention ratio），用于动态衡量模型当前的认知焦点。基于该指标，构建AGS框架：对感知token自适应触发潜在（latent）推理，在连续空间中保留高保真视觉信息；对逻辑token强制执行显式文本生成，以维持结构锚定。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法达到最先进性能，显著提升了准确率和推理效率，减少了自回归步数和延迟。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过注意力量化认知焦点并据此动态切换潜在推理与显式推理，可以有效解耦多模态推理中的感知与逻辑过程，在无需训练的前提下兼顾性能与效率。

Abstract: Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) requires both fine-grained visual perception and rigorous logical deduction. Explicit text-based Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is computationally expensive and prone to visual hallucinations, while existing latent reasoning methods typically require costly training. Furthermore, directly adapting training-free LLM reasoning mechanisms to the multimodal setting yields unstable performance. We identify that this failure stems from their reliance on token-level entropy, which fundamentally conflates perceptual ambiguity (e.g., unclear visual details) with logical uncertainty (e.g., complex reasoning steps). To overcome this bottleneck, we present a novel training-free inference strategy for MLLMs that explicitly decouples perception and reasoning. We propose a novel metric, the vision-to-text attention ratio, to dynamically gauge the model's cognitive focus. Guided by this metric, our proposed framework, Attention-Guided Switching (AGS), adaptively triggers latent reasoning for perceptual tokens to preserve high-fidelity visual information in the continuous space, while enforcing explicit text generation for logical tokens to maintain structural anchoring. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, significantly improving both accuracy and inference efficiency by reducing autoregressive steps and latency. Code is released at https://github.com/swordAndSnow/MM26-AGS.

</details>


### [171] [Adaptive Modality Reliability Diagnosis and Restoration for Robust Multimodal Intent Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03475)
*Suraj Kumar,Mohnish Raj,Soumi Chattopadhayay,Chandranath Adak,Ayan Dutta*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: PRIME是一个闭环可靠性引导框架，通过诊断-修复-重评估的流程处理多模态意图识别中的模态质量退化问题，在保持干净数据性能的同时显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态意图识别方法隐式推断模态重要性，通过重新加权或抑制不可靠输入来处理噪声模态，但未考虑退化的模态是否可以修复并重新信任。这导致信息被简单丢弃而非恢复利用。

Method: PRIME包含三个核心组件：(1) 通过上下文对数方差表示各模态弱点，融合预测置信度、认知分歧、跨模态共识和特征退化等诊断证据；(2) 使用可控模态损坏训练可靠性估计器（基于异方差不确定性目标）；(3) 基于原型条件变分恢复模块从互补模态重建退化表示，并在修复后重新评估可靠性，最终采用逆方差多模态融合。

Result: 在多模态意图识别基准上，PRIME在干净数据下保持竞争力性能，同时在模态缺失、噪声、语义冲突和模态不平衡等条件下显著提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过"先修复再决断"的闭环思路，PRIME验证了退化模态经过修复后可以被重新信任并贡献预测，优于直接抑制或丢弃不可靠模态的现有方法。

Abstract: Multimodal intent recognition combines linguistic, acoustic, and visual evidence, but individual modalities may be noisy, missing, semantically conflicting, or disproportionately dominant. Existing methods typically infer modality importance implicitly and either reweight or suppress unreliable inputs, without determining whether a degraded modality can be repaired and subsequently trusted. We propose PRIME (Precision-weighted Reliability Inference and Modality rEstoration), a closed-loop reliability guided framework that jointly diagnoses, restores, and reassesses modality quality at the sample level. PRIME represents the weakness of each modality through a contextual log-variance estimated from complementary diagnostic evidence, including predictive confidence, epistemic disagreement, cross-modal consensus, and feature degeneracy.
