Because modality-reliability annotations are unavailable, the estimator is explicitly trained using controlled modality corruption with known degradation severity, together with a heteroscedastic uncertainty objective. Rather than directly discarding an unreliable modality, PRIME uses its estimated weakness to control a prototype-conditioned variational restoration module that reconstructs the degraded representation from complementary modalities. Crucially, reliability is re-estimated after restoration, allowing the model to determine whether the repaired representation has become sufficiently trustworthy to contribute to prediction. The resulting post-restoration precisions are used for inverse-variance multimodal fusion. Experiments on multimodal intent-recognition benchmarks show that PRIME maintains competitive clean-data performance while improving robustness under missing, noisy, conflicting, and modality-imbalanced conditions.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Extracting ODRL Policies from Business Process Models: A Graph Traversal Approach to Compliance-by-Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02607)
*Meem Arafat Manab,Marinella Quaranta,Ilaria Angela Amantea,Sheyla Leyva-Sánchez,Víctor Rodríguez-Doncel*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动从BPMN XML中提取ODRL政策管道的方案，利用BPMN控制流天然编码的义务逻辑模态（obligation/permission/prohibition），通过可达性检查分类任务，并创新性地将中间捕获事件建模为带有解除约束的禁止规则，实现合规即提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织维护大量BPMN流程模型，但其中的规范内容（义务、权限、禁止）无法被政策基础设施访问。ODRL是机器可读政策的标准语言，但大规模编写ODRL耗时且依赖专家，而大语言模型生成存在结构无效风险。需要一个自动化管道将BPMN模型直接转换为ODRL政策。

Method: 管道遍历BPMN流程图，通过可达性检查将每个任务分类为odrl:Duty或odrl:Permission，并对中间捕获事件引入新颖处理，将其建模为带解除约束的odrl:Prohibition规则，以捕获等待语义（这是先前方法忽略的）。

Result: 实现了合规即提取（compliance-by-extraction）方案，可直接从组织已有的BPMN流程模型中派生可审计、可互操作的ODRL政策。提供了开源实现和在线演示。

Conclusion: 该方法弥合了BPMN流程模型与ODRL政策基础设施之间的鸿沟，使规范内容能够自动化地转化为机器可读的政策，提高了政策生成的效率和可靠性。

Abstract: Organisations maintain large corpora of process models expressed in the Business Process Model and Notation (BPMN), yet the normative content encoded in those models, the obligations, permissions, and prohibitions that govern participant behaviour, remains inaccessible to policy infrastructure. The Open Digital Rights Language (ODRL) is the emerging lingua franca of machine-readable policy, but authoring ODRL at scale is slow, expert-intensive work, and generation by large language models introduces well-documented risks of structural invalidity. We present a pipeline that resolves this gap by extracting ODRL policies automatically from BPMN XML, grounded in the observation that BPMN control flow encodes deontic modalities by construction. The pipeline traverses the BPMN process graph, classifies each task as an odrl:Duty or odrl:Permission via a reachability check, and introduces a novel treatment of intermediate catch events as odrl:Prohibition rules with lifting constraints, capturing waiting semantics that prior approaches have dropped. The result is a compliance-by-extraction approach in which auditable, interoperable ODRL policies are derived directly from the process models organisations already maintain. The implementation and a live demonstration are available at https://github.com/manabcodes/bpmn2odrl and https://bpmn.linkeddata.es/.

</details>


### [173] [Scalable Exact Densest P-Partite Subgraph Search in Heterogeneous Information Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03061)
*Jiadong Xie,Jiaming Yang,Kangfei Zhao,Jeffrey Xu Yu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出BoxDPpS，一种高效的精确算法用于解决异质信息网络中的最密P部子图搜索（DPpS）问题，通过盒级搜索、安全区域剪枝、去冗余表示和有界预热等技术，在保持精确最优解的同时实现平均27.04倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质信息网络（HIN）中稠密的跨类型结构能揭示有意义的语义模式，但现有精确求解DPpS问题的方法受限于候选iRM集数量庞大以及反复求解大规模辅助网络的高计算成本，可扩展性不足。

Method: 提出BoxDPpS精确方法，包含四个关键技术：1）盒级搜索与安全区域剪枝，减少搜索空间；2）消除同一iRM集的冗余表示；3）通过有界预热改进早期剪枝；4）压缩每个固定M的辅助网络，实现精确参数化伪流求解。

Result: 在七个真实数据集上的实验表明，BoxDPpS保持DPpS精确最优解，同时相比现有最先进方法实现平均27.04倍的加速。

Conclusion: BoxDPpS通过多维度的成本削减策略，显著提升了DPpS问题的精确求解效率，为异质信息网络中的稠密子图分析提供了更实用的工具。

Abstract: Heterogeneous information networks (HINs) model typed entities and typed relations, where dense cross-type structures can reveal cohesive semantic patterns such as prolific author-paper-venue groups. Given a query meta-path, the densest P-partite subgraph search (DPpS) problem jointly selects a nonempty vertex set at each typed position and maximizes the number of induced meta-path instances normalized by the geometric mean of the selected set sizes. Existing exact methods solve DPpS by searching over iRM-sets and reducing each fixed-M problem to minimum-cut computations. However, their scalability is limited by the large number of candidate iRM-sets and the high cost of repeatedly solving large auxiliary networks. In this paper, we propose BoxDPpS, an efficient exact approach that reduces both sources of cost. It performs box-level search with safe region pruning, eliminates redundant representations of the same iRM-set, improves early pruning through bounded warm-up, and compresses each fixed-M auxiliary network for exact parametric pseudoflow solving. Experiments on seven real-world datasets show that BoxDPpS preserves the exact DPpS optimum while achieving an average speedup of 27.04x over the state-of-the-art method.

</details>


### [174] [SieveIVF: Threshold-Aware IVF Execution for Large-Scale Training Data Deduplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03199)
*Zhisheng Hu,Zhifang Li,Junjie Chen,Ke Xu,Yuxuan Li,Chufeng Chen,Rui Chen,Zhe Chen,Ming-Chang Yang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SieveIVF是一个阈值感知的IVF执行器，利用"连续W次搜索无合格候选即停止"的规则来减少嵌入训练数据去重中的计算开销，在Lance中实现后相比固定探针IVF在不同工作负载上获得4.1–8.4倍加速，同时保持极低的召回率损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入训练数据去重需要在相似度阈值之上检索候选重复边，但固定探针的倒排文件（IVF）搜索忽略了这一应用谓词，给每个查询分配相同的分区预算，导致计算浪费。观察发现合格邻居出现得早但搜索深度差异大，迫切需要阈值感知的自适应搜索策略来优化工作分配。

Method: 提出SieveIVF，一个阈值感知的IVF执行器。核心策略是连续W次搜索无合格候选即停止（stopping rule）。为保持分区优先的批处理效率，采用连续批处理（continuous batching）按分区分组就绪查询，并在此之上叠加前瞻调度器（lookahead scheduler），只暴露停止规则已承诺的工作来提高并发度，且不改变停止决策或返回结果。

Result: 在W=8时，SieveIVF在四个10M Hunyuan工作负载上比固定探针IVF快4.1–7.6倍，在两个公开100M工作负载上快6.1–8.4倍（索引和搜索参数相同）。池化过滤后的top-10召回率损失在Hunyuan上仅0.03–1.13个百分点，公开工作负载上为1.43–2.29个百分点。

Conclusion: SieveIVF证明应用谓词可以引导IVF的工作分配，无需改变索引结构或有界top-k接口。通过停止规则和前瞻调度，在保持批处理效率的同时大幅提升性能，且召回率损失可接受。

Abstract: Embedding-based training data deduplication retrieves candidate duplicate edges above an application similarity threshold, but fixed-probe inverted-file (IVF) search ignores this predicate when giving every query the same partition budget. Across four Hunyuan workloads, qualifying neighbors appear early despite sharply varying search depths. We present SieveIVF, a threshold-aware IVF executor that stops after $W$ consecutive searches find no qualifying candidate. The systems challenge is to preserve partition-major batching when each query's remaining work depends on prior results. Continuous batching groups ready queries by partition. A lookahead scheduler layers on top, exposing only work committed by the stopping rule to increase concurrency without changing stopping decisions or returned results. We implement SieveIVF in Lance. At $W=8$, SieveIVF is $4.1$--$7.6\times$ faster than fixed-probe IVF on four 10M Hunyuan workloads and $6.1$--$8.4\times$ faster on two public 100M workloads under the same index and search parameters, with pooled filtered top-10 recall losses of $0.03$--$1.13$ percentage points on Hunyuan and $1.43$--$2.29$ percentage points on the public workloads. These results show how an application predicate can guide IVF work allocation without changing the index or bounded top-$k$ interface.

</details>


### [175] [Getting to the Root: A Combined Complexity Perspective on Consistent Query Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03477)
*Miika Hannula*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了在主键约束下布尔自连接免费合取查询的一致查询回答的组合复杂度问题。作者聚焦于无环攻击图且一元主键的查询场景，提出了key-nonkey图的概念，证明当图中源强连通分量数量有界时问题可在多项式时间内解决，否则问题为Pi_2^P-完全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一致查询回答的数据复杂度在过去二十年已被广泛研究，但其组合复杂度受到的关注较少。本文旨在识别使该问题在计算上易于处理的查询特定结构特征，填补组合复杂度研究的空白。

Method: 针对一元主键下的布尔自连接免费合取查询，利用key-nonkey有向图刻画查询结构。提出变量守卫、攻击传播图等新概念，并发展数据库与查询饱和、查询规范化等技术，同时分析源强连通分量数量对复杂度的影响。

Result: 证明了在所述假设下，当key-nonkey图中源强连通分量数量有常数界限时，一致查询回答的组合复杂度为多项式时间；若无界限制，则该问题的组合复杂度为Pi_2^P-完全。

Conclusion: 该工作首次对一致查询回答的组合复杂度进行了系统刻画，通过源强连通分量数量这一关键结构性指标界定了多项式时间可解与Pi_2^P-完全之间的复杂度分界线。

Abstract: The data complexity of consistent query answering has been extensively studied over the past two decades. In this paper, we study its combined complexity, which has gained less attention. The input is a triple consisting of a set of integrity constraints, a (potentially inconsistent) database, and a query, and the question is to determine whether all repairs of the database with respect to the constraints satisfy the query. Our aim is to isolate query-specific structural features that make this problem computationally tractable. We focus on Boolean self-join-free conjunctive queries over primary-key constraints, as in this context the corresponding data complexity problem has been completely classified. Moreover, we restrict attention to queries with an acyclic attack graph, which are precisely those queries that admit a first-order rewriting. As an additional simplifying assumption, we focus on unary primary keys. Each conjunctive query with unary primary keys naturally gives rise to a directed graph, which we call the key-nonkey graph, where each atom contributes an edge from its key variable to each of its non-key variables. Our main result is that, under the stated assumptions, the combined complexity of consistent query answering is in polynomial time whenever the number of source strongly connected components (SCCs) in the key-nonkey graph is bounded by a constant. To prove this result, we develop novel concepts, such as variable guarding and attack-propagation graphs, and techniques, such as database and query saturation and query normalization. In addition, without the boundedness restriction, the combined complexity of this problem is shown to be Pi_2^P-complete.

</details>


### [176] [RAG-Stack: Co-Optimizing RAG Serving Performance and Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03487)
*Haiqiang Zhang,Yuanqing Lei,Wanting Li,Tao Zhang,Wenqi Jiang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: RAG-Stack是一个用于高效发现RAG系统质量-性能Pareto前沿的框架，由三个组件组成：RAG-PE（迭代设计空间探索算法）、RAG-IR（工作负载抽象）和RAG-CM（性能预测模型），能够在无需部署每个候选配置的情况下搜索联合算法-系统配置空间，并可将现有Pareto前沿迁移到新的服务系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代RAG系统存在许多配置选择（检索索引、模型选择、检索调用方式等），每种配置在答案质量和服务性能之间存在不同权衡，难以针对特定应用部署选择最优设置。

Method: RAG-Stack包含三个核心组件：RAG-PE用于迭代设计空间探索，选择下一个要评估的RAG配置；RAG-IR提供不同RAG算法的工作负载抽象；RAG-CM构建性能模型，预测给定硬件上的最优部署和服务性能。三者协同实现在不部署每个候选配置的情况下搜索联合算法-系统配置空间，并支持将既有Pareto前沿迁移到新服务系统。

Result: 在相同优化迭代次数下，跨多样数据集的Pareto前沿覆盖了归一化质量-性能空间的52.5%到153.2%，优于在相同RAG设计空间上评估的最先进配置搜索方法。

Conclusion: RAG-Stack能够高效发现更优的RAG配置Pareto前沿，显著提升对质量-性能空间的覆盖率，为多样化的RAG应用和部署场景提供有效的配置优化方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), which augments large language model (LLM) generation with information retrieved from databases, has become a widely used approach for knowledge-intensive applications. Modern RAG systems, however, expose many configuration choices, such as retrieval indexes, model selections, and how models invoke retrieval. Each configuration yields a different trade-off between answer quality and serving performance, making it challenging to choose the optimal setting for a specific application deployment. We present RAG-Stack, a framework for efficiently discovering quality-performance Pareto frontiers across diverse RAG applications and serving systems. RAG-Stack consists of RAG-PE, an iterative design-space exploration algorithm that selects the next RAG configuration to evaluate; RAG-IR, a workload abstraction for diverse RAG algorithms; and RAG-CM, a performance model that predicts the optimal deployment and serving performance on the given hardware. Together, these components allow RAG-Stack to search the joint algorithm-system configuration space without deploying every candidate and to transfer an existing Pareto frontier to a new serving system. Given the same number of optimization iterations across diverse datasets, the Pareto frontiers found by RAG-Stack cover 52.5% to 153.2% more of the normalized quality-performance space than those found by state-of-the-art configuration-search methods evaluated over the same RAG design space.

</details>


### [177] [Evaluating LLMs in Database Scenarios: A Lifecycle Benchmark for Assessing Their Potential in Core Database Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03794)
*Shunfan Zheng,Dongsheng Shi,Yue Li,Xin Yi,Linlin Wang,Gerard de Melo*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了DBLifeBench，这是首个覆盖数据库完整生命周期（设计、实现、操作、调试、维护）五个关键阶段的LLM评估基准，并引入Progressive-Text2SQL任务来模拟人类迭代解决问题的认知过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估基准过度聚焦于Text-to-SQL任务，忽略了数据库管理的完整生命周期，无法全面衡量LLM在真实数据库管理场景中的多样化能力。

Method: 构建DBLifeBench基准，覆盖五个关键生命周期阶段；提出Progressive-Text2SQL新型任务，利用结构化推理图谱模拟人类迭代式问题解决过程。

Result: 通用模型的性能表现均衡，而专门的Text-to-SQL模型在非编码阶段（如设计和维护）出现"灾难性遗忘"现象。

Conclusion: DBLifeBench为评估和构建真正的全栈数据库智能奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are transforming database interaction paradigms, evolving from simple query translators to autonomous database administrators (DBAs). However, current evaluation benchmarks remain disproportionately fixated on Text-to-SQL tasks, neglecting the holistic Database Lifecycle-from initial schema design to post-deployment maintenance. This narrow focus fails to capture the diverse capabilities required for real-world database management. To bridge this gap, we introduce DBLifeBench, the first benchmark to evaluate LLMs across five critical lifecycle phases: Design, Implementation, Operation, Debugging, and Maintenance. Furthermore, addressing the cognitive mismatch between ambiguous natural language and complex SQL logic, we propose Progressive-Text2SQL, a novel task utilizing structured reasoning graphs to mimic human iterative problem-solving. Our extensive evaluation reveals a critical insight: while general-purpose models demonstrate balanced performance, specialized Text-to-SQL models suffer from ``catastrophic forgetting'' in non-coding phases like design and maintenance. DBLifeBench serves as a foundational step toward evaluating and building true full-stack database intelligence.

</details>


### [178] [Uplifting the Superpowers of Worst-Case-Optimal Join Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03840)
*Adrián Gómez-Brandón,Aidan Hogan,Gonzalo Navarro*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出将过滤条件（selections/filters）原生集成到最坏情况最优（wco）连接算法中，取代传统的前置或后置过滤方法，并通过扩展紧凑索引Ring来支持属性图处理，实验证明该方法在效率上优于多个基线系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代图查询语言（如SPARQL和GQL）以基本图模式（BGP）为核心，但还包含过滤条件等其他特性。传统方法通过前置或后置过滤来处理这些条件，存在效率瓶颈。作者希望将过滤条件原生融入wco连接算法以提高查询效率。

Method: 将过滤条件下推并原生集成到wco连接算法中，而非通过前置/后置过滤处理。具体地，扩展了Ring这一紧凑索引结构，使其在保持空间紧凑性的同时，利用新的wco技术原生处理属性图中的过滤条件。

Result: 实验结果表明，该扩展方法在性能上优于多种基线系统，证明了将过滤条件原生融入wco连接算法的优越性。

Conclusion: 将过滤条件原生集成到wco连接算法中能够显著提升图查询效率，并且可以在保持紧凑索引结构的同时实现这一改进。

Abstract: Worst-case-optimal (wco) join algorithms have demonstrated their power -- in both theory and practice -- to efficiently solve complex Basic Graph Patterns (BGPs). Modern graph query languages, such as SPARQL and GQL, have BGPs at their core, but also have a wide range of other features, including filters (aka.\ selections). Such conditions are typically handled via pre- or post-filtering, before or after processing the BGPs. In this paper we show how to uplift wco join algorithms so as to incorporate such filtering natively, improving efficiency. We demonstrate the superiority of this approach by extending the \textit{Ring} -- a compact index that provides wco resolution of BGPs within almost no extra space on top of the graph -- so as to handle property graphs using our new techniques while retaining compactness. We implement this extension and experimentally show that it outperforms various baseline systems.

</details>
